📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMJ open  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-24  |  DOI: 10.1136/bmjopen-2026-121924  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 16, 9, e121924

多模态检测 radically treated 高危型HPV 阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌早期复发:一项前瞻性多中心 UK 队列研究 研究(HERD)的方案

Multimodality detection of early relapse in radically treated high-risk HPV-negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma: study protocol for a prospective multicentre UK cohort study (HERD).
作者： Sanjena Mithra, Ahmed Abdullah Ahmed, Amit Roshan, Paul R Barber, Camila de Carvalho Gomes, Ton Coolen, Elinor Sawyer, Nitzan Rosenfeld, Ralph Sinkus, Miguel Reis-Ferreira
摘要中文翻译INTRODUCTION: 头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)是全球第七常见的癌症,每年在英国约有超过12,000例新发病例。尽管采用积极的 radical 治疗 治疗,约50%的局部晚期(III/IV期)人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)阴性患者在2年内复发,通常在第一年内。虽然目前的 post-治疗 随访计划已被临床指南推荐,但影像学 监测 主要依赖于症状在初始3个月后进行,且缺乏可靠的早期复发生物标志物。因此,复发常被发现得太晚,无法进行手术挽救,尽管在某些情况下及时手术可将生存率提高多达73%。Head and Neck Early Relapse Detection Study 是一项大规模的前瞻性研究,旨在整合影像学、基因组学、免疫学和微生物组数据集,以对这一高风险患者群体进行复发风险分层和 post-治疗 监测。METHODS AND ANALYSIS: 设计:前瞻性、观察性 队列研究 研究。SETTING: 英国的二级和三级 NHS 医院。PARTICIPANTS: 招募最多200名 III/IV期(高风险)HPV 阴性 HNSCC 患者。参与者将被纳入发现组(n=100)和验证组(n=100)。患有 HPV 阳性肿瘤、转移性疾病或其他在方案中定义的禁忌症的患者将被排除。INTERVENTIONS: 观察性研究,无实验干预。参与者将在2年内接受随访,纵向收集血液、唾液、尿液、粪便、新鲜和存档肿瘤组织以及尽可能进行的先进MRI,同时继续其标准临床护理。PRIMARY OUTCOMES: 开发并独立验证多模态复发风险预测模型。SECONDARY OUTCOMES: 识别用于 primary 治疗 分层的患者群体,建立纵向生物样本库,并确定复发的生物学机制。多模态数据将通过机器学习和贝叶斯建模进行整合。ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: 伦理批准由 Bromley 研究伦理委员会(21/PR/1581)授予。研究结果将通过同行评审的出版物、会议报告和科学会议进行传播。TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT05097625.
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决对 高危型HPV 阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)患者早期复发的可靠生物标志物的需求,鉴于其高复发率以及当前 post-治疗 监测 的局限性。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性、观察性 队列研究 研究,涉及英国二级和三级 NHS 医院最多200名 III/IV期 HPV 阴性 HNSCC 患者,重点是开发和验证多模态复发风险预测模型。
核心方法
本研究将通过机器学习和贝叶斯建模整合影像学、基因组学、免疫学和微生物组数据集,以开发复发风险预测模型。
主要结果
本研究旨在识别用于 primary 治疗 分层的患者群体,建立纵向生物样本库,并确定复发的生物学机制,研究结果将通过同行评审的出版物、会议报告和科学会议进行传播。
解释与创新
本研究的创新之处在于整合多种数据模态,以提高 高危型HPV 阴性 HNSCC 患者复发风险预测和分层的准确性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能有助于更有效的 post-治疗 监测,并实现患者分层,从而潜在地提高生存率,使及时的手术干预成为可能。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括缺乏随机化以及可能存在的混杂因素,但其设计旨在为复发风险预测和患者分层提供有价值的见解。

Multimodality detection of early relapse in radically treated high-risk HPV-negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma: study protocol for a prospective multicentre UK cohort study (HERD).

Authors: Sanjena Mithra, Ahmed Abdullah Ahmed, Amit Roshan, Paul R Barber, Camila de Carvalho Gomes, Ton Coolen, Elinor Sawyer, Nitzan Rosenfeld, Ralph Sinkus, Miguel Reis-Ferreira
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) is the seventh most common cancer globally, with over 12 000 new cases annually in the UK. Despite aggressive radical treatment, approximately 50% of patients with locally advanced (stage III/IV), human papilloma virus (HPV)-negative disease relapse within 2 years, often in the first year. Although current post-treatment follow-up schedules are recommended by clinical guidelines, imaging surveillance is led by symptoms after the initial 3 months and reliable biomarkers for early relapse are lacking. Consequently, recurrences are frequently detected too late for surgical salvage, though timely surgery can improve survival by up to 73% in selected cases. Head and Neck Early Relapse Detection Study represents a large-scale, prospective effort to integrate imaging, genomic, immunologic and microbiome datasets for relapse risk stratification and post-treatment surveillance in this high-risk patient group. METHODS AND ANALYSIS: Design: Prospective, observational cohort study. SETTING: Secondary and tertiary NHS hospitals in the UK. PARTICIPANTS: Recruiting up to 200 patients with stage III/IV (high-risk) HPV-negative HNSCC. Participants are enrolled into discovery (n=100) and validation (n=100) cohorts. Patients with HPV-positive tumours, metastatic disease or other contraindications defined in the protocol will be excluded. INTERVENTIONS: Observational study with no experimental intervention. Participants are followed for 2 years with longitudinal collection of blood, saliva, urine, stool, fresh and archival tumour tissue and advanced MRI where possible in addition to their standard of clinical care. PRIMARY OUTCOMES: Development and independent validation of a multimodal recurrence risk prediction model. SECONDARY OUTCOMES: Identifying patient populations for primary treatment stratification, establishment of a longitudinal biorepository and identifying biological mechanisms of recurrence. Multimodal data will be integrated using machine learning and Bayesian modelling. ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: Ethical approval was granted by the Bromley Research Ethics Committee (21/PR/1581). Findings will be disseminated through peer-reviewed publications, conference presentations and scientific meetings. TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT05097625.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for reliable biomarkers for early relapse in high-risk HPV-negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC), given the high recurrence rate and the current limitations in post-treatment surveillance.
Design and population
A prospective, observational cohort study involving up to 200 patients with stage III/IV HPV-negative HNSCC from secondary and tertiary NHS hospitals in the UK, with a focus on developing and validating a multimodal recurrence risk prediction model.
Core methods
The study will integrate imaging, genomic, immunologic, and microbiome datasets using machine learning and Bayesian modelling to develop a recurrence risk prediction model.
Main results
The study aims to identify patient populations for primary treatment stratification, establish a longitudinal biorepository, and determine biological mechanisms of recurrence, with findings to be disseminated through peer-reviewed publications, conference presentations, and scientific meetings.
Interpretation and novelty
The study's novel approach involves the integration of multiple data modalities to improve relapse risk prediction and stratification in high-risk HPV-negative HNSCC patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could lead to more effective post-treatment surveillance and stratification of patients, potentially improving survival rates by enabling timely surgical interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the lack of randomization and the potential for confounding factors, but it is designed to provide valuable insights into relapse risk prediction and patient stratification.

预测父母对人乳头瘤病毒疫苗接种犹豫:开发和内部验证机器学习模型

Predicting parental Human papillomavirus vaccine hesitancy: development and internal validation of machine learning models
作者： Israel Oluwamayomikun Alabi, Daniel Biftu Bekalo, Christiana Kartsonaki, Valirie Ndip Agbor
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种是喀麦隆女性中第二常见的癌症。然而,高程度的父母对HPV疫苗接种的犹豫阻碍了消除进程,且目前尚无经过验证的预测工具。本研究旨在开发并内部验证用于预测Buea卫生区父母对HPV疫苗接种犹豫的机器学习模型,并确定关键预测因素。方法 本研究采用二次分析方法,使用了来自1,156名9至18岁儿童父母的横断面调查数据。犹豫程度来源于自我报告的疫苗接种状态和意愿。通过组LASSO筛选了26个预测组(17个用于逻辑回归;26个用于树模型)。数据按80/20的比例分为训练集(n=925)和测试集(n=231)。逻辑回归、随机森林和XGBoost模型通过重复交叉验证进行调优,阈值由Youden’s J统计量设定。在测试集上评估了模型的区分度、校准度和Brier分数。使用SHAP对预测结果进行了解释。结果 所有模型的区分度均相当(AUC-ROC 0.841–0.870)。XGBoost的AUC最高(0.870,与逻辑回归的0.869相比,DeLong p=0.965);逻辑回归的敏感度最高(73.1%),F1分数最高(74.9%),且Brier分数最低(0.1435)。所有模型的校准斜率均超过1(1.12–1.46)。信息不足、对HPV疫苗接种的安全性感知不足以及对卫生部的不信任是主要的预测因素。讨论 三种模型均实现了相当且具有临床意义的区分度,算法复杂性并未在标准回归之上提高预测能力。犹豫程度更强烈地由信息获取和机构信任预测,而非社会人口学劣势。结论 这些发现支持通过建立信任的参与方式,而非广泛的人口学宣传活动,来应对父母对HPV疫苗接种的犹豫。已有知识 由于父母对HPV疫苗接种的犹豫是HPV疫苗接种的主要障碍,但撒哈拉以南非洲地区的预测建模证据有限,且该地区尚未开发出内部验证的父母对HPV疫苗接种犹豫预测模型。本研究的贡献 我们开发并内部验证了逻辑回归、随机森林和XGBoost模型,用于预测父母对HPV疫苗接种的犹豫。所有三种模型均显示出相当的区分度(AUC-ROC 0.841–0.870),其中逻辑回归实现了最低的Brier分数和最高的敏感度及F1分数。信息不足、对HPV疫苗接种的安全性感知不足以及对卫生部的不信任是所有模型和可解释性分析中的主要预测因素。对研究、实践或政策的潜在影响 这些发现支持开发和外部验证简单且可解释的预测工具,以识别可能从有针对性的信任建立HPV疫苗接种沟通和社区参与中受益的父母,从而支持HPV疫苗接种和宫颈癌预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种是喀麦隆女性中第二常见的癌症。然而,高程度的父母对HPV疫苗接种的犹豫阻碍了消除进程,且目前尚无经过验证的预测工具。
设计与对象
本研究旨在开发并内部验证用于预测Buea卫生区父母对HPV疫苗接种犹豫的机器学习模型,并确定关键预测因素。数据按80/20的比例分为训练集(n=925)和测试集(n=231)。
核心方法
方法 本研究采用二次分析方法,使用了来自1,156名9至18岁儿童父母的横断面调查数据。通过组LASSO筛选了26个预测组(17个用于逻辑回归;26个用于树模型)。逻辑回归、随机森林和XGBoost模型通过重复交叉验证进行调优,阈值由Youden’s J统计量设定。
主要结果
结果 所有模型的区分度均相当(AUC-ROC 0.841–0.870)。XGBoost的AUC最高(0.870,与逻辑回归的0.869相比,DeLong p=0.965);逻辑回归的敏感度最高(73.1%),F1分数最高(74.9%),且Brier分数最低(0.1435)。所有模型的校准斜率均超过1(1.12–1.46)。
解释与创新
犹豫程度更强烈地由信息获取和机构信任预测,而非社会人口学劣势。信息不足、对HPV疫苗接种的安全性感知不足以及对卫生部的不信任是所有模型和可解释性分析中的主要预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持开发和外部验证简单且可解释的预测工具,以识别可能从有针对性的信任建立HPV疫苗接种沟通和社区参与中受益的父母,从而支持HPV疫苗接种和宫颈癌预防。
局限与证据强度
三种模型均实现了相当且具有临床意义的区分度,算法复杂性并未在标准回归之上提高预测能力。

Predicting parental Human papillomavirus vaccine hesitancy: development and internal validation of machine learning models

Authors: Israel Oluwamayomikun Alabi, Daniel Biftu Bekalo, Christiana Kartsonaki, Valirie Ndip Agbor
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objectives Cervical cancer is Cameroon’s second most common cancer among women. However, high parental Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine hesitancy impedes elimination, and no validated predictive tool exists. This study aimed to develop and internally validate machine learning models predicting parental HPV vaccine hesitancy in the Buea Health District and identify key predictors. Methods This secondary analysis used cross-sectional survey data from 1,156 parents of children aged 9–18 years. Hesitancy was derived from self-reported vaccination status and intention. Twenty-six predictor groups were screened via group LASSO (17 retained for logistic regression; all 26 for tree-based models). Data were split 80/20 into training (n=925) and test (n=231) sets. Logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost were tuned via repeated cross-validation while thresholds were set by Youden’s J statistic. Discrimination, calibration, and Brier score were assessed on the test set. Predictions were interpreted using SHAP. Results All models showed comparable discrimination (AUC-ROC 0.841–0.870). XGBoost had the highest AUC (0.870, vs 0.869 for logistic regression, DeLong p=0.965); logistic regression had the highest sensitivity (73.1%), F1-score (74.9%), and lowest Brier score (0.1435). Calibration slopes exceeded 1 (1.12–1.46) across models. Insufficient vaccine information, perceived vaccine unsafety, and distrust in the Ministry of Health were the dominant predictors. Discussion The three models achieved comparable, clinically meaningful discrimination, and algorithmic complexity did not improve prediction over standard regression. Hesitancy was more strongly predicted by information access and institutional trust than sociodemographic disadvantage. Conclusion These findings support trust-building engagement over broad demographic campaigns. What is already known on this topic Parental HPV vaccine hesitancy is a major barrier to HPV vaccination, but predictive modelling evidence from sub-Saharan Africa is limited and no internally validated model for parental HPV vaccine hesitancy has been developed in the region. What this study adds We developed and internally validated logistic regression, random forest and XGBoost models for predicting parental HPV vaccine hesitancy, with all three showing comparable discrimination (AUC-ROC 0.841–0.870) and logistic regression achieving the lowest Brier score and highest sensitivity and F1-score. Insufficient vaccine information, perceived vaccine unsafety and distrust in the Ministry of Health were the dominant predictors across the models and interpretability analyses. How this study might affect research, practice or policy The findings support the development and external validation of simple, interpretable prediction tools to identify parents who may benefit from targeted, trust-building vaccine communication and community engagement to support HPV vaccination and cervical cancer prevention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is Cameroon’s second most common cancer among women. However, high parental Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine hesitancy impedes elimination, and no validated predictive tool exists.
Design and population
This study aimed to develop and internally validate machine learning models predicting parental HPV vaccine hesitancy in the Buea Health District and identify key predictors. Data were split 80/20 into training (n=925) and test (n=231) sets.
Core methods
Methods This secondary analysis used cross-sectional survey data from 1,156 parents of children aged 9–18 years. Twenty-six predictor groups were screened via group LASSO (17 retained for logistic regression; all 26 for tree-based models). Logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost were tuned via repeated cross-validation while thresholds were set by Youden’s J statistic.
Main results
Results All models showed comparable discrimination (AUC-ROC 0.841–0.870). XGBoost had the highest AUC (0.870, vs 0.869 for logistic regression, DeLong p=0.965); logistic regression had the highest sensitivity (73.1%), F1-score (74.9%), and lowest Brier score (0.1435). Calibration slopes exceeded 1 (1.12–1.46) across models.
Interpretation and novelty
Hesitancy was more strongly predicted by information access and institutional trust than sociodemographic disadvantage. Insufficient vaccine information, perceived vaccine unsafety, and distrust in the Ministry of Health were the dominant predictors across the models and interpretability analyses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the development and external validation of simple, interpretable prediction tools to identify parents who may benefit from targeted, trust-building vaccine communication and community engagement to support HPV vaccination and cervical cancer prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The three models achieved comparable, clinically meaningful discrimination, and algorithmic complexity did not improve prediction over standard regression.

回顾性研究:万代汤联合重组人干扰素α-2b在持续高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染患者中的临床效果分析

Retrospective Study of Wandai Decoction Plus Recombinant Human Interferon Alpha-2b in Persistent High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection.
作者： Xiao-Yun He
摘要中文翻译本回顾性研究评估了万代汤(WDT)联合重组人干扰素α-2b(rhIFN-α-2b)在持续高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染且具有脾虚证型的患者中的临床结果,特别关注阴道微生态和外周T细胞亚群。分析了2024年3月至2025年9月期间接受治疗的125名患者的临床数据。根据治疗方案,57名患者接受了口服万代汤联合rhIFN-α-2b阴道栓剂治疗,而68名患者仅接受了rhIFN-α-2b治疗。两组患者均接受了三疗程的连续治疗。治疗后,观察组的总有效率高于对照组(89.47% vs. 72.06%,p = 0.015),且首次HPV转阴时间更短(P = 0.028),但总体四类HPV结果分布(P = 0.060)和HPV16/18特异性转阴率(P = 0.138)无显著差异。更有利的阴道微生态结果包括阴道pH值较低、白细胞酯酶阳性率和胺试验阳性率较低,以及正常过氧化氢状态比例和阴道清洁度I-II级比例较高,乳酸杆菌优势比例也较高(P < 0.05)。真菌检测、细菌性阴道病相关异常和混合感染的发生率也较低(P < 0.05)。治疗后,观察组的CD4+ T细胞百分比和CD4+/CD8+比值更高,而CD8+ T细胞百分比更低(P < 0.05)。总体中医证候积分、异常宫颈颜色和接触性出血等指标也显示出更有利的结果。两组记录的不良事件总体发生率无显著差异(P = 0.057)。万代汤联合rhIFN-α-2b与更优的HPV相关、阴道微生态、外周T细胞亚群和症状结果相关;然而,回顾性设计和残留混杂因素限制了因果解释的结论。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估万代汤(WDT)联合重组人干扰素α-2b(rhIFN-α-2b)在持续高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染且具有脾虚证型的患者中的临床效果,特别关注阴道微生态和外周T细胞亚群。
设计与对象
本回顾性研究分析了2024年3月至2025年9月期间接受治疗的125名患者的临床数据,其中57名患者接受了万代汤联合rhIFN-α-2b阴道栓剂治疗,而68名患者仅接受了rhIFN-α-2b治疗。
核心方法
本研究通过分析患者数据评估临床结果,包括HPV转阴率、阴道微生态参数、外周T细胞亚群和症状改善情况,两组患者均接受了三疗程的连续治疗。
主要结果
观察组的总有效率高于对照组(89.47% vs. 72.06%,p = 0.015),且首次HPV转阴时间更短(P = 0.028),但总体四类HPV结果分布(P = 0.060)和HPV16/18特异性转阴率(P = 0.138)无显著差异。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,万代汤联合rhIFN-α-2b可能改善持续高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染患者的临床结果,特别是在HPV转阴和阴道微生态方面,但回顾性设计限制了因果结论的得出。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来关于整合治疗的高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染研究提供参考,并有助于理解传统中医药在管理HPV相关疾病中的作用。
局限与证据强度
回顾性设计和潜在的残留混杂因素限制了证据的强度,因此在没有明确因果结论的情况下应谨慎解释这些发现。

Retrospective Study of Wandai Decoction Plus Recombinant Human Interferon Alpha-2b in Persistent High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection.

Authors: Xiao-Yun He
Original abstract (English)
This retrospective study evaluated clinical outcomes associated with Wandai Decoction (WDT) plus recombinant human interferon α-2b (rhIFN-α-2b) in patients with persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection and a spleen-deficiency pattern, with particular attention to vaginal microecology and peripheral T-cell subsets. Clinical data from 125 patients treated between March 2024 and September 2025 were analyzed. According to the treatment regimen, 57 patients received oral WDT plus rhIFN-α-2b vaginal suppositories, whereas 68 patients received rhIFN-α-2b alone. Both groups received three consecutive treatment courses. After treatment, the observation group had a higher total effective rate than the control group (89.47% vs. 72.06%, p = 0.015) and a shorter time to first HPV conversion (P = 0.028), whereas the overall four-category HPV outcome distribution (P = 0.060) and HPV16/18-specific conversion rate (P = 0.138) did not differ significantly. More favorable vaginal microecological findings included lower pH, leukocyte esterase positivity, and amine test positivity, along with higher rates of normal hydrogen peroxide status, vaginal cleanliness grade I-II, and Lactobacillus predominance (P < 0.05). Fungal detection, bacterial vaginosis-related abnormalities, and mixed infection were also less frequent (P < 0.05). Post-treatment CD4+ T-cell percentage and the CD4+/CD8+ ratio were higher, whereas CD8+ T-cell percentage was lower, in the observation group (P < 0.05). The total traditional Chinese medicine syndrome score, abnormal cervical color, and contact bleeding also showed more favorable findings. The overall rate of recorded adverse events did not differ significantly between groups (P = 0.057). WDT plus rhIFN-α-2b was associated with more favorable HPV-related, vaginal microecological, peripheral T-cell subset, and symptom outcomes; however, the retrospective design and residual confounding preclude causal interpretation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the clinical outcomes of using Wandai Decoction (WDT) plus recombinant human interferon α-2b (rhIFN-α-2b) in patients with persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection and a spleen-deficiency pattern, focusing on vaginal microecology and peripheral T-cell subsets.
Design and population
A retrospective study analyzed clinical data from 125 patients treated between March 2024 and September 2025, with 57 patients receiving WDT plus rhIFN-α-2b vaginal suppositories and 68 patients receiving rhIFN-α-2b alone.
Core methods
The study involved evaluating clinical outcomes through analysis of patient data, including HPV conversion rates, vaginal microecological parameters, peripheral T-cell subsets, and symptom improvements, with both groups receiving three consecutive treatment courses.
Main results
The observation group had a higher total effective rate (89.47% vs. 72.06%, p = 0.015) and a shorter time to first HPV conversion (P = 0.028) compared to the control group, while the overall four-category HPV outcome distribution (P = 0.060) and HPV16/18-specific conversion rate (P = 0.138) did not differ significantly.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that WDT plus rhIFN-α-2b may improve clinical outcomes in patients with persistent high-risk HPV infection, particularly in terms of HPV conversion and vaginal microecology, though the retrospective design limits causal conclusions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform future studies on integrative treatments for persistent high-risk HPV infection and contribute to understanding the role of traditional Chinese medicine in managing HPV-related conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective design and potential residual confounding limit the strength of the evidence, and the findings should be interpreted cautiously without definitive causal conclusions.

2006-2025年间全球政策对低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗引进的影响

Global policy determinants of national human papillomavirus vaccine introductions in 2006-2025.
作者： Jiyeon Suh, Marc Brisson, Emily A. Burger, Jane J. Kim, Jean‐François Laprise, Mark Jit
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管安全有效的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗自2006年起已可获得,但低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)的国家引进进展缓慢。HPV 疫苗的采用可能受到世界卫生组织(WHO)不断变化的建议、Gavi疫苗联盟的资助政策以及2019冠状病毒病(COVID-19)大流行带来的干扰的影响。然而,这些因素的相对贡献尚未量化。研究设计与方法:本研究分析了2006年至2025年间LMICs国家HPV 疫苗引进率的政策决定因素,采用:(i)广义线性模型(GLM)评估预定政策事件,(ii)变化点分析以识别采用趋势的转变。结果:在一系列探讨不同事件和结果变量的GLM中,WHO于2022年提出的单剂接种建议与国家引进数量的增加最为相关,而Gavi对HPV 疫苗的资助政策扩展(2017-2025)在某些模型中也具有重要意义。将包含和不包含单剂接种计划的模型预测进行比较,估计在LMICs中额外有22.46个国家引进了HPV 疫苗。变化点分析发现2022年引进数量显著增加。结论:这些结果量化了HPV 疫苗全球采用的主要驱动因素,为扩大公平的疫苗可及性提供了可操作的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管安全有效的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗自2006年起已可获得,但低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)的国家引进进展缓慢。HPV 疫苗的采用可能受到世界卫生组织(WHO)不断变化的建议、Gavi疫苗联盟的资助政策以及2019冠状病毒病(COVID-19)大流行带来的干扰的影响。
设计与对象
本研究分析了2006年至2025年间LMICs国家HPV 疫苗引进率的政策决定因素。
核心方法
本研究采用广义线性模型(GLM)评估预定政策事件,并使用变化点分析以识别采用趋势的转变。
主要结果
在一系列探讨不同事件和结果变量的GLM中,WHO于2022年提出的单剂接种建议与国家引进数量的增加最为相关,而Gavi对HPV 疫苗的资助政策扩展(2017-2025)在某些模型中也具有重要意义。将包含和不包含单剂接种计划的模型预测进行比较,估计在LMICs中额外有22.46个国家引进了HPV 疫苗。变化点分析发现2022年引进数量显著增加。
解释与创新
这些结果量化了HPV 疫苗全球采用的主要驱动因素,为扩大公平的疫苗可及性提供了可操作的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为扩大公平的疫苗可及性提供了可操作的见解,并有助于制定策略以加速LMICs中HPV 疫苗的引进。
局限与证据强度
本研究未报告局限性,但其证据基于模型预测和变化点分析,可能存在固有的不确定性。

Global policy determinants of national human papillomavirus vaccine introductions in 2006-2025.

Authors: Jiyeon Suh, Marc Brisson, Emily A. Burger, Jane J. Kim, Jean‐François Laprise, Mark Jit
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Although safe and effective human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines have been available since 2006, national introduction in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) has progressed slowly. HPV vaccine adoption may have been shaped by evolving World Health Organization (WHO) recommendations, funding policies of Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance, and the disruption from the Coronavirus Disease 2019 pandemic. However, their relative contributions have not been quantified. RESEARCH DESIGN AND METHODS: This study analyzed policy determinants of national HPV vaccine introduction rate in LMICs from 2006 to 2025, using: (i) generalized linear model (GLM) to assess predetermined policy events, and (ii) changepoint analysis to identify shifts in adoption trends. RESULTS: Across a range of GLMs exploring different events and outcome variables, WHO's one-dose recommendation in 2022 was most strongly associated with an increase in country introductions, with the expansion of Gavi's funding policy for HPV vaccines (2017-2025) also important in some models. Comparing model projections with and without the one-dose schedule yielded an estimated 22.46 additional national HPV vaccine introductions in LMICs. Changepoint analysis identified a significant increase in introductions in 2022. CONCLUSIONS: These results quantify the primary global drivers of HPV vaccine uptake, offering actionable insights for expanding equitable vaccine access.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Although safe and effective human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines have been available since 2006, national introduction in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) has progressed slowly. HPV vaccine adoption may have been shaped by evolving World Health Organization (WHO) recommendations, funding policies of Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance, and the disruption from the Coronavirus Disease 2019 pandemic.
Design and population
This study analyzed policy determinants of national HPV vaccine introduction rate in LMICs from 2006 to 2025.
Core methods
This study used generalized linear model (GLM) to assess predetermined policy events and changepoint analysis to identify shifts in adoption trends.
Main results
Across a range of GLMs exploring different events and outcome variables, WHO's one-dose recommendation in 2022 was most strongly associated with an increase in country introductions, with the expansion of Gavi's funding policy for HPV vaccines (2017-2025) also important in some models. Comparing model projections with and without the one-dose schedule yielded an estimated 22.46 additional national HPV vaccine introductions in LMICs. Changepoint analysis identified a significant increase in introductions in 2022.
Interpretation and novelty
These results quantify the primary global drivers of HPV vaccine uptake, offering actionable insights for expanding equitable vaccine access.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer actionable insights for expanding equitable vaccine access and inform strategies to accelerate HPV vaccine introduction in LMICs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on model projections and changepoint analysis, which may have inherent uncertainties.

自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒 DNA检测的可接受性和偏好性与医生采集的巴氏涂片比较

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.
作者： Ourlad Alzeus G. Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N. de Guzman, Amina C. Kunting, Sheriah Laine M. de Paz-Silava, Michele H. Diwa, Ian Kim B. Tabios, Sheriah Laine M. de Paz‐Silava
摘要中文翻译背景:自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。本研究旨在评估女性对自我采集阴道样本进行HPV DNA检测与巴氏涂片的感知、体验、意愿和偏好。方法:在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。使用五点李克特量表问卷在自我采集前后测量感知。采用描述性统计分析,并使用配对和独立Wilcoxon检验比较李克特量表回答。使用Cochran's Q和McNemar检验分析接受筛查的意愿。参与者偏好的筛查方法的原因被主题分类,并以堆叠条形图显示。结果:自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。结论:自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现可以为提高HPV DNA检测参与率和扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性提供策略依据,强调了对自我采集方法进行教育和支持的必要性。局限性与证据强度:本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和自我报告数据,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度中等,因为它提供了关于可接受性和偏好的见解,但并未建立因果关系。

问题与背景
自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为提高HPV DNA检测参与率和扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性提供策略依据,强调了对自我采集方法进行教育和支持的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和自我报告数据,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度中等,因为它提供了关于可接受性和偏好的见解,但并未建立因果关系。

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.

Authors: Ourlad Alzeus G. Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N. de Guzman, Amina C. Kunting, Sheriah Laine M. de Paz-Silava, Michele H. Diwa, Ian Kim B. Tabios, Sheriah Laine M. de Paz‐Silava
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited. This study aimed to assess women's perceptions, experiences, willingness and preferences regarding self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing compared with Pap smears. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing. Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests. Participants' reasons for their preferred screening method were categorized thematically and displayed in stacked bar charts. RESULTS: Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness. CONCLUSIONS: Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing.
Core methods
Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests. Participants' reasons for their preferred screening method were categorized thematically and displayed in stacked bar charts.
Main results
Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness.
Interpretation and novelty
Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to improve the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines by highlighting the need for education and support for self-collection methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it provides insights into acceptability and preferences but does not establish causal relationships.

塞拉利昂接受HIV治疗的青少年女孩照顾者对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的认知和态度:基于医疗机构的横断面研究

Caregiver knowledge and Attitudes toward Human Papillomavirus Vaccination among adolescent girls living with HIV in Sierra Leone: A health facility-based cross-sectional study
作者： Darlinda Fatmata Jiba, Mamadu Baldeh, Patrick Turay, Saidu Kanu, Matilda N. Kamara, Lynda M. L Farma-Grant, Umu Barrie, Waheed O Awonuga, Mary M Baio, Daniel Sesay
摘要中文翻译目的:评估塞拉利昂接受HIV治疗的青少年女孩照顾者对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的认知、意愿及其障碍。方法:本横断面研究在塞拉利昂的公立三级医院和高流量HIV诊所中,通过结构化问卷调查了249名照顾者对HPV的认知、意愿及其障碍。数据通过卡方检验、逻辑回归分析和Kruskal-Wallis检验进行统计分析,以评估相关性。结果:仅有32.1%(80/249)的照顾者听说过HPV,更少的人了解其与宫颈癌(24.4%,60/246)或预防方法(31.9%,79/248)之间的关联。存在普遍的误解,其中16.3%(36/221)的照顾者错误地将口服避孕药与宫颈癌联系起来,而61.1%(44/72)不愿接种疫苗的照顾者则表示对疫苗有效性的不信任。尽管受过高等教育的照顾者表现出最高的知识水平(45.5%,p < 0.001),但他们的疫苗接种意愿(50%,33/66)与受教育程度较低的群体相似,凸显了知识与行动之间的差距。结构性障碍占主导地位,其中66.1%(154/233)的照顾者表示不了解疫苗,10.3%(24/233)错过了学校安排的疫苗接种日。关系动态显著影响了疫苗接种意愿:扩展家庭成员表现出最高的意愿(69.4%,p = 0.029),而祖父母则意愿最低(31%,9/29)。尽管系统性障碍如疫苗接种服务中断、疫苗供应和冷链限制了疫苗接种率,但医疗工作者作为信息来源被证明是最有效的(秩和 = 889.5,p = 0.039)。结论:本研究强调了需要进行文化定制的教育以消除误解,并采用社区驱动的策略,通过医疗工作者和扩展家庭等值得信赖的行动者来提高疫苗接种率。本研究支持将HPV疫苗接种和宫颈癌筛查整合到HIV治疗服务中的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估塞拉利昂接受HIV治疗的青少年女孩照顾者对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种的认知、意愿及其障碍,以填补对这些因素如何影响该特定人群疫苗接种决策的理解空白。
设计与对象
本研究在塞拉利昂的249名接受HIV治疗的青少年女孩照顾者中进行,是一项基于医疗机构的横断面研究,重点考察他们对HPV疫苗接种的认知和态度。
核心方法
通过结构化问卷在公立三级医院和高流量HIV诊所中收集数据,统计分析包括卡方检验、逻辑回归分析和Kruskal-Wallis检验,以评估相关性。
主要结果
仅有32.1%(80/249)的照顾者听说过HPV,更少的人了解其与宫颈癌(24.4%,60/246)或预防方法(31.9%,79/248)之间的关联。存在普遍的误解,其中16.3%(36/221)的照顾者错误地将口服避孕药与宫颈癌联系起来,而61.1%(44/72)不愿接种疫苗的照顾者则表示对疫苗有效性的不信任。
解释与创新
本研究揭示了显著的知识与行动之间的差距,尽管受过高等教育的照顾者具有最高的知识水平(45.5%,p < 0.001),但他们的疫苗接种意愿(50%,33/66)与受教育程度较低的群体相似,表明知识本身并不足以转化为行动。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了需要进行文化定制的教育以消除误解并提高疫苗接种率,同时也支持将HPV疫苗接种和宫颈癌筛查整合到HIV治疗服务中的必要性。
局限与证据强度
结构性障碍,如对疫苗的不了解和错过学校安排的疫苗接种日,较为突出,且由于研究采用横断面设计,可能存在自我报告偏差,因此证据强度中等。

Caregiver knowledge and Attitudes toward Human Papillomavirus Vaccination among adolescent girls living with HIV in Sierra Leone: A health facility-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Darlinda Fatmata Jiba, Mamadu Baldeh, Patrick Turay, Saidu Kanu, Matilda N. Kamara, Lynda M. L Farma-Grant, Umu Barrie, Waheed O Awonuga, Mary M Baio, Daniel Sesay
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To assess the knowledge, willingness and barriers to human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination among caregivers of adolescent girls living with HIV in Sierra Leone. Methods This cross-sectional study examined HPV vaccine knowledge, willingness, and barriers among 249 caregivers of adolescent girls living with HIV in Sierra Leone. Data were collected through structured questionnaires at public tertiary hospitals and high-volume HIV clinics, and associations were assessed using chi-square tests, logistic regression, and Kruskal-Wallis analyses. Results Only 32.1% (80/249) of caregivers had heard of HPV, with even fewer aware of its link to cervical cancer (24.4%, 60/246) or prevention methods of cervical cancer (31.9%, 79/248). Misconceptions were prevalent; 16.3% (36/221) erroneously linked oral contraceptives to cervical cancer, and 61.1% (44/72) of caregivers unwilling to vaccinate cited distrust in vaccine efficacy. While tertiary-educated caregivers exhibited the highest level of knowledge (45.5%, p < 0.001), their willingness to vaccinate (50%, 33/66) was comparable to that of less-educated groups, highlighting a knowledge-action gap. Structural barriers dominated, with 66.1% (154/233) citing unawareness of the vaccine and 10.3% (24/233) missing school-based vaccination days. Relationship dynamics significantly influenced willingness: extended family members showed the highest willingness (69.4%, p = 0.029), whereas grandparents were least willing (31%, 9/29). Although systemic gaps such disrupted vaccination services, vaccines supplies and cold chain limited uptake, healthcare workers emerged as the most effective information source (rank sum = 889.5, p = 0.039). Conclusion The study highlights needs for culturally tailored education to dispel myths, community-driven strategies engaging trusted actors like healthcare workers and extended families.This study justifies the need for integration of HPV vaccination and cervical cancer screening into HIV care services.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate caregiver knowledge, willingness, and barriers to human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination among adolescent girls living with HIV in Sierra Leone, addressing the gap in understanding how these factors influence vaccination decisions in this specific population.
Design and population
A health facility-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 249 caregivers of adolescent girls living with HIV in Sierra Leone, focusing on their awareness and attitudes toward HPV vaccination.
Core methods
Structured questionnaires were used to collect data at public tertiary hospitals and high-volume HIV clinics, with statistical analyses including chi-square tests, logistic regression, and Kruskal-Wallis tests to assess associations.
Main results
Only 32.1% (80/249) of caregivers had heard of HPV, and fewer were aware of its link to cervical cancer (24.4%, 60/246) or prevention methods (31.9%, 79/248). Misconceptions were common, with 16.3% (36/221) linking oral contraceptives to cervical cancer, and 61.1% (44/72) of caregivers unwilling to vaccinate citing distrust in vaccine efficacy.
Interpretation and novelty
The study reveals a significant knowledge-action gap, as tertiary-educated caregivers had the highest knowledge but similar willingness to vaccinate as less-educated groups, indicating that knowledge alone does not translate to action.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for culturally tailored education to address misconceptions and improve vaccination uptake, as well as the integration of HPV vaccination and cervical cancer screening into HIV care services.
Limitations and evidence strength
Structural barriers, such as unawareness of the vaccine and missed vaccination days, were prominent, and the evidence is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential for self-reporting bias.

磷酸化的人乳头瘤病毒HPV-16 E7通过vangl1调控失调破坏宫颈癌细胞的平面细胞极性。

Phosphorylated HPV‐16 E7 disrupts planar cell polarity in cervical cancer cells through vangl1 dysregulation.
作者： Ifeoluwa D. Gbala, Om Basukala, Amira Zine El Abidine, Michael P. Myers, Rebecca Bertolio, Giannino Del Sal, Lawrence Banks
摘要中文翻译高危型(HR)人乳头瘤病毒(HPVs)依赖于E6和E7癌蛋白的持续表达来重塑上皮生物学,驱动并维持恶性转化。高危型E7癌蛋白具有多种功能,其由酪蛋白激酶II(CKII)介导的磷酸化是关键的翻译后修饰,增强了其致癌潜能。因此,有必要全面阐明E7磷酸化对HPV驱动的转化和恶性进展的影响。本研究发现,Vangl1是一种核心的平面细胞极性(PCP)支架蛋白,是HPV-16 E7的一种新型、依赖磷酸化的相互作用蛋白。通过蛋白质组学、生化和细胞实验,本研究证实CKII介导的磷酸化对于Vangl1的招募是必需的,并且这种相互作用在HPV-16 E7中更为显著,揭示了高危型HPVs可能通过干扰上皮组织结构的新机制。在CaSki细胞和异位表达模型中,E7的表达显著破坏了Vangl1的稳态,导致双相的蛋白质稳态失衡,其中磷酸化的Vangl1异常稳定。这种保留与E7的稳定性相呼应,揭示了一种相互依赖的致癌性稳定机制。此外,我们发现,在E7存在的情况下,Vangl1在细胞骨架部分被耗尽,并且E7会转移到膜区室,与Vangl1共定位。与此一致,我们发现这种异常的Vangl1稳定化源于E7对适配蛋白相关蛋白复合体1亚基Mu 1(AP1M1)的干扰所导致的Vangl1定位异常和运输受损。功能上,Vangl1 siRNA介导的敲除在CaSki球体中模拟了E6/E7的缺失,导致球体结构破坏、侵袭性降低以及对化疗压力的敏感性增加。这些表型表明Vangl1是E7重塑上皮行为的关键下游效应因子。综上所述,我们的研究揭示了HPV-16 E7通过CKII依赖机制劫持核心平面极性成分,从而破坏上皮结构并促进恶性特征;因此,将Vangl1定位为HPV相关宫颈癌的潜在可药靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解磷酸化的人乳头瘤病毒HPV-16 E7如何通过Vangl1调控失调破坏平面细胞极性(PCP),以阐明E7磷酸化对HPV驱动的转化和恶性进展的影响。
设计与对象
本研究使用CaSki细胞和异位表达模型来研究HPV-16 E7与Vangl1的相互作用,重点关注CKII介导的磷酸化对Vangl1稳态和上皮组织结构的影响。
核心方法
使用蛋白质组学、生化和细胞实验来建立CKII磷酸化的人乳头瘤病毒HPV-16 E7与Vangl1的相互作用,并评估E7对Vangl1稳态和上皮结构的影响。
主要结果
本研究发现,CKII介导的HPV-16 E7磷酸化对于Vangl1的招募是必需的,导致双相的蛋白质稳态失衡,其中磷酸化的Vangl1异常稳定。E7的表达破坏了Vangl1的稳态,而Vangl1 siRNA介导的敲除在CaSki球体中模拟了E6/E7的缺失,导致球体结构破坏、侵袭性降低以及对化疗压力的敏感性增加。
解释与创新
本研究识别了Vangl1作为HPV-16 E7的一种新型、依赖磷酸化的相互作用蛋白,揭示了高危型HPVs可能通过干扰上皮组织结构的新机制。这一发现将Vangl1定位为HPV相关宫颈癌的潜在可药靶点。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对Vangl1可能是一种治疗HPV相关宫颈癌的策略,因为E7介导的Vangl1稳定化破坏了上皮结构并促进了恶性特征。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括使用单一细胞系和异位表达模型,这些可能无法完全代表体内条件的复杂性。由于依赖于体外模型,证据强度中等。

Phosphorylated HPV‐16 E7 disrupts planar cell polarity in cervical cancer cells through vangl1 dysregulation.

Authors: Ifeoluwa D. Gbala, Om Basukala, Amira Zine El Abidine, Michael P. Myers, Rebecca Bertolio, Giannino Del Sal, Lawrence Banks
Original abstract (English)
High‐risk (HR) human papillomaviruses (HPVs) rely on the continuous expression of the E6 and E7 oncoproteins to reshape epithelial biology, drive and sustain malignant transformation. The high‐risk E7 oncoprotein is multifunctional, and its phosphorylation by Casein Kinase II (CKII) is a key post translational modification that amplifies its oncogenic potential. Therefore, it is important to extensively delineate the consequences of E7 phosphorylation on HPV-driven transformation and malignant progression. Here, we identify Vangl1, a core planar cell polarity (PCP) scaffold protein, as a novel, phosphorylation‐dependent interactor of HPV‐16 E7. Through proteomics, biochemical and cell-based assays, this study established that CKII‐mediated phosphorylation is required for Vangl1 recruitment, and that this interaction is more pronounced with HPV‐16 E7, revealing a new mechanism by which HR HPVs might disrupt epithelial organization. In CaSki cells and ectopic expression models, E7 expression profoundly disrupts Vangl1 homeostasis, creating a biphasic proteostatic imbalance in which phosphorylated Vangl1 is aberrantly stabilised. This retention parallels E7 stability, revealing a reciprocal oncogenic stabilization. Furthermore, we found that Vangl1 is depleted in cytoskeletal cell fractions in the presence of E7, and that E7 translocates to membrane compartments where it colocalizes with Vangl1. In line with this, we identified that this abnormal Vangl1 stabilization occurs from its mislocalization and impaired trafficking arising from E7's interference with the Adaptor-Related Protein Complex 1 Subunit Mu 1 (AP1M1). Functionally, Vangl1 siRNA-mediated ablation in CaSki spheroids mirrors E6/E7 loss, leading to disrupted spheroidal architecture, reduced invasion, and increased sensitivity to chemotherapeutic stress. These phenotypes position Vangl1 as a key downstream effector through which E7 reshaped epithelial behaviour. Taken together, our findings reveal a CKII-dependent mechanism by which HPV-16 E7 hijacks a core planar polarity component to destabilize epithelial structure and promote malignant traits; thus, positioning Vangl1 as a potential druggable target in HPV-associated cervical cancer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of phosphorylated HPV-16 E7 in disrupting planar cell polarity (PCP) through Vangl1 dysregulation, addressing the need to delineate the consequences of E7 phosphorylation on HPV-driven transformation and malignant progression.
Design and population
The study involved CaSki cells and ectopic expression models to investigate the interaction between HPV-16 E7 and Vangl1, focusing on the effects of CKII-mediated phosphorylation on Vangl1 homeostasis and epithelial organization.
Core methods
Proteomics, biochemical, and cell-based assays were used to establish the interaction between CKII-phosphorylated HPV-16 E7 and Vangl1, and to assess the effects of E7 on Vangl1 homeostasis and epithelial structure.
Main results
The study found that CKII-mediated phosphorylation of HPV-16 E7 is required for Vangl1 recruitment, leading to a biphasic proteostatic imbalance where phosphorylated Vangl1 is aberrantly stabilized. E7 expression disrupts Vangl1 homeostasis, and Vangl1 siRNA-mediated ablation in CaSki spheroids mimics E6/E7 loss, leading to disrupted spheroidal architecture, reduced invasion, and increased sensitivity to chemotherapeutic stress.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified Vangl1 as a novel, phosphorylation-dependent interactor of HPV-16 E7, revealing a new mechanism by which HR HPVs might disrupt epithelial organization. This finding positions Vangl1 as a potential druggable target in HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting Vangl1 could be a therapeutic strategy for HPV-associated cervical cancer, as Vangl1's stabilization by E7 disrupts epithelial structure and promotes malignant traits.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a single cell line and ectopic expression models, which may not fully represent the complexity of in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on in vitro models.

山东省中国2017年8月至2025年10月期间人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗上市后的实际安全数据

Real-world safety data of human papillomavirus vaccines after marketing in Shandong, China, August 2017–October 2025
作者： Meng Xie, Han Li, Xiaofei Qiu, Xiao Kang, Jun Wei, Shaonan Liu, Zexin Tao, Yingjie Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗在中国上市后的安全性,以应对随着疫苗接种接种量增加而日益增长的安全性需求。
设计与对象
2017年8月至2025年10月期间,在中国山东省开展了一项实际安全性研究,利用了中国国家AEFI信息管理系统(CNAEFIs)和山东省免疫接种项目信息系统(SDIPIs)的数据。
核心方法
采用单变量泊松回归模型探讨与HPV 疫苗接种后不良事件相关的风险因素,并进行了不均衡性分析以检测异常信号。
主要结果
From August 2017 to October 2025, 1056 AEFI cases were reported, with an overall rate of 7.99 per 100,000 doses. Of these, 1008 cases (95.46%) were mild vaccine-related reactions, and 15 cases (1.42%) were rare adverse reactions. Myalgia, induration with a diameter greater than 5 cm, immune thrombocytopenia, and mild reactions such as fatigue, drowsiness, dizziness, and headache were detected as abnormal signals.
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明HPV 疫苗的AEFI报告率较低,且其安全性主要表现为轻度,这是在实际上市后数据背景下的一项新发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强化HPV 疫苗的安全性来支持公共卫生策略,并有助于继续推进疫苗接种计划以预防与HPV相关的疾病,如宫颈癌。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖被动报告系统,这可能导致罕见不良事件被低估,且由于数据的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Real-world safety data of human papillomavirus vaccines after marketing in Shandong, China, August 2017–October 2025

Authors: Meng Xie, Han Li, Xiaofei Qiu, Xiao Kang, Jun Wei, Shaonan Liu, Zexin Tao, Yingjie Zhang
Original abstract (English)
After the Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine was listed for use in China in 2017, it has proven to be a safe and cost-effective method for preventing HPV infection. With the increase in vaccination volume, its safety is becoming increasingly important. The safety data from post-marketing can enhance public confidence in vaccines. We collected adverse events following immunization (AEFI) through the Chinese National AEFI Information Management System (CNAEFIs) between August 2017 and October 2025. Vaccination data from the same period were obtained via the Shandong Provincial Immunization Program Information Systems (SDIPIs). A Univariate Poisson regression model was used to explore risk factors associated with HPV vaccine AEFI. Disproportionality analysis was performed to detect abnormal signals related to the HPV vaccine. From August 2017 to October 2025 in Shandong, CNAEFIs received 1056 AEFI cases, with an overall reporting AEFI rate of 7.99 per 100,000 doses. 1008 cases (95.46%) were mild vaccine-related reactions, and 15 cases (1.42%) were rare adverse reactions. Myalgia, induration with a diameter greater than 5 cm, immune thrombocytopenia, and mild reactions such as fatigue, drowsiness, dizziness, and headache were detected as abnormal signals. The reporting AEFI rate of the HPV vaccine in Shandong was low, and the majority of AEFIs were mild cases. The safety profile in post-marketing was good.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the safety of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines after their introduction in China, addressing the growing need to ensure their safety as vaccination volumes increased.
Design and population
A real-world safety study was conducted in Shandong, China, between August 2017 and October 2025, utilizing data from the Chinese National AEFI Information Management System (CNAEFIs) and the Shandong Provincial Immunization Program Information Systems (SDIPIs).
Core methods
A Univariate Poisson regression model was used to explore risk factors associated with HPV vaccine adverse events following immunization (AEFI), and disproportionality analysis was performed to detect abnormal signals.
Main results
From August 2017 to October 2025, 1056 AEFI cases were reported, with an overall rate of 7.99 per 100,000 doses. Of these, 1008 cases (95.46%) were mild vaccine-related reactions, and 15 cases (1.42%) were rare adverse reactions. Myalgia, induration with a diameter greater than 5 cm, immune thrombocytopenia, and mild reactions such as fatigue, drowsiness, dizziness, and headache were detected as abnormal signals.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a low reporting rate of AEFI and a predominantly mild safety profile for the HPV vaccine, which is a novel observation in the context of real-world post-marketing data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies by reinforcing the safety of HPV vaccines and supporting continued vaccination programs to prevent HPV-associated diseases such as cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on passive reporting systems, which may underrepresent rare adverse events, and the evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the data.

白沙瓦医生对人乳头瘤病毒知识及其推荐HPV 疫苗接种给年轻女孩的意愿:横断面研究

Knowledge of Human Papillomavirus and Willingness to Recommend HPV Vaccination for Young Girls Among Doctors in Peshawar: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Anum Fatima Qazi, Khalid Rehman1, Ali Khan2, Bisma Fayyaz1
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染是导致宫颈癌的主要原因,这在低收入和中等收入国家仍然是重要的公共卫生问题。HPV 疫苗接种是预防HPV相关疾病的有效策略,但在巴基斯坦,疫苗的接种率仍然有限。医疗保健提供者在向适龄青少年传播HPV 疫苗接种的益处和推荐该疫苗方面可以发挥重要作用。随着开伯尔-普什图省准备实施HPV 疫苗,评估医生对HPV相关知识及其推荐意愿具有重要意义。本研究旨在评估巴基斯坦白沙瓦医生对HPV相关知识及其推荐HPV 疫苗接种的意愿。方法:本研究是一项基于医院的横断面研究,于2025年8月至2026年1月期间在白沙瓦的公立医疗机构中对189名注册医生进行。参与者完成了评估社会人口学特征、HPV感染及疫苗接种知识,以及推荐疫苗意愿的结构化、自我管理问卷。知识得分被分为良好、中等和较差三类。数据通过描述性统计、关联性检验和多变量二元逻辑回归进行分析。报告了调整后的比值比(ORs)及其95%置信区间(CIs)。结果:平均知识得分为12.71 ± 3.01,其中121名参与者(64.0%)具有良好的知识。总体而言,144名参与者(76.2%)表示愿意推荐HPV 疫苗接种。接受过正式HPV培训的医生平均知识得分高于未接受过培训的医生(13.97 ± 1.84 vs. 12.38 ± 3.17;p < 0.001)。报告了调整后的比值比(ORs)及其95%置信区间(CIs)。解释与创新性:尽管整体多变量模型的解释力有限,但HPV知识水平较高与推荐疫苗接种的调整后比值比更高相关。白沙瓦的大多数医生展示了良好的HPV相关知识,并表示有较高的意愿推荐HPV 疫苗接种。科学与公共卫生意义:加强HPV相关教育,结合明确的临床和实施指导,可能有助于医生在巴基斯坦扩大疫苗接种项目时向适龄青少年传播该疫苗的信息。局限性与证据强度:整体多变量模型的解释力有限,且本研究仅在单一城市进行,这可能限制其推广性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染是导致宫颈癌的主要原因,这在低收入和中等收入国家仍然是重要的公共卫生问题。HPV 疫苗接种是预防HPV相关疾病的有效策略,但在巴基斯坦,疫苗的接种率仍然有限。
设计与对象
本研究是一项基于医院的横断面研究,于2025年8月至2026年1月期间在白沙瓦的公立医疗机构中对189名注册医生进行。参与者完成了评估社会人口学特征、HPV感染及疫苗接种知识,以及推荐疫苗意愿的结构化、自我管理问卷。
核心方法
知识得分被分为良好、中等和较差三类。数据通过描述性统计、关联性检验和多变量二元逻辑回归进行分析。
主要结果
平均知识得分为12.71 ± 3.01,其中121名参与者(64.0%)具有良好的知识。总体而言,144名参与者(76.2%)表示愿意推荐HPV 疫苗接种。接受过正式HPV培训的医生平均知识得分高于未接受过培训的医生(13.97 ± 1.84 vs. 12.38 ± 3.17;p < 0.001)。报告了调整后的比值比(ORs)及其95%置信区间(CIs)。
解释与创新
尽管整体多变量模型的解释力有限,但HPV知识水平较高与推荐疫苗接种的调整后比值比更高相关。白沙瓦的大多数医生展示了良好的HPV相关知识,并表示有较高的意愿推荐HPV 疫苗接种。
科研与公卫意义
加强HPV相关教育,结合明确的临床和实施指导,可能有助于医生在巴基斯坦扩大疫苗接种项目时向适龄青少年传播该疫苗的信息。
局限与证据强度
整体多变量模型的解释力有限,且本研究仅在单一城市进行,这可能限制其推广性。

Knowledge of Human Papillomavirus and Willingness to Recommend HPV Vaccination for Young Girls Among Doctors in Peshawar: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Anum Fatima Qazi, Khalid Rehman1, Ali Khan2, Bisma Fayyaz1
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, which remains an important public health problem in low- and middle-income countries. HPV vaccination is an effective strategy for preventing HPV-related disease, but vaccine uptake in Pakistan remains limited. Healthcare providers can play an important role in communicating the benefits of HPV vaccination and recommending vaccination to eligible adolescents. As Khyber Pakhtunkhwa prepares for HPV vaccine implementation, assessing physicians' knowledge and willingness to recommend vaccination is important. This study aimed to assess HPV-related knowledge and willingness to recommend HPV vaccination among doctors in Peshawar, Pakistan. Methods: A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from August 2025 to January 2026 among 189 registered doctors in public sector hospitals in Peshawar. Participants completed a structured, self-administered questionnaire assessing sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge of HPV infection and vaccination, and willingness to recommend the vaccine. Knowledge scores were categorised as good, moderate, or poor. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics, tests of association, and multivariable binary logistic regression. Adjusted odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were reported. Results: The mean knowledge score was 12.71 ± 3.01, and 121 participants (64.0%) had good knowledge. Overall, 144 participants (76.2%) reported willingness to recommend HPV vaccination. Doctors who had received formal HPV training had higher mean knowledge scores than those without prior training (13.97 ± 1.84 vs. 12.38 ± 3.17; p Conclusion: Most doctors in Peshawar demonstrated good HPV-related knowledge and reported a high willingness to recommend HPV vaccination. Higher HPV knowledge was associated with greater adjusted odds of willingness to recommend vaccination, although the overall multivariable model had limited explanatory power. Strengthening HPV-related education, together with clear clinical and implementation guidance, may support physicians in communicating HPV vaccination to eligible adolescents as vaccination programmes expand in Pakistan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, which remains an important public health problem in low- and middle-income countries. HPV vaccination is an effective strategy for preventing HPV-related disease, but vaccine uptake in Pakistan remains limited.
Design and population
A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from August 2025 to January 2026 among 189 registered doctors in public sector hospitals in Peshawar. Participants completed a structured, self-administered questionnaire assessing sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge of HPV infection and vaccination, and willingness to recommend the vaccine.
Core methods
Knowledge scores were categorised as good, moderate, or poor. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics, tests of association, and multivariable binary logistic regression.
Main results
The mean knowledge score was 12.71 ± 3.01, and 121 participants (64.0%) had good knowledge. Overall, 144 participants (76.2%) reported willingness to recommend HPV vaccination. Doctors who had received formal HPV training had higher mean knowledge scores than those without prior training (13.97 ± 1.84 vs. 12.38 ± 3.17; p < 0.001). Adjusted odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
Higher HPV knowledge was associated with greater adjusted odds of willingness to recommend vaccination, although the overall multivariable model had limited explanatory power. Most doctors in Peshawar demonstrated good HPV-related knowledge and reported a high willingness to recommend HPV vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening HPV-related education, together with clear clinical and implementation guidance, may support physicians in communicating HPV vaccination to eligible adolescents as vaccination programmes expand in Pakistan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The overall multivariable model had limited explanatory power, and the study was conducted in a single city, which may limit generalisability.

影响阿尔伯塔省学校为基础的人乳头瘤病毒疫苗接种的因素:一项定性研究

Factors influencing human papillomavirus school-based immunization in Alberta: a qualitative inquiry
作者： Hannah Sell, Jennifer Malkin, Lisa Allen Scott, Ann M. Toohey, Lisa Allen-Scott
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 尽管人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)感染及其相关癌症可通过疫苗接种预防,但阿尔伯塔省及加拿大其他地区针对学龄男孩和女孩的疫苗接种率仍不理想。为弥合这一接种差距并为阿尔伯塔省的学校为基础的HPV疫苗接种计划提供信息,本研究旨在了解疫苗接种的障碍和促进因素,这些因素在不同健康区和人口群体中的差异,以及总结克服这些障碍的建议。方法 与阿尔伯塔省健康者共同参与的Healthier Together小组成员进行了虚拟访谈,这些成员是学龄儿童(11-17岁)的父母或监护人。另外,还邀请了计划相关利益相关者(如领导者和一线工作人员)参加虚拟焦点小组。研究材料的开发和数据分析由健康护理可及性概念框架指导。使用模板主题分析法对访谈和焦点小组数据进行分析。结果 HPV疫苗接种的促进因素包括学校为基础的计划的便利性、将疫苗与其他常规疫苗一同推广、与学校、父母/监护人和社区建立关系、包含个人故事的信息传播以及青少年参与。障碍包括对HPV知识的缺乏、对信任和透明度的感知缺失、与性活动的关联、接种同意流程、语言障碍以及接种焦虑。关键建议包括改善HPV信息传播以解决知识差距和误解、改变计划材料的分发方式、采用减少接种焦虑的策略,以及继续与父母/监护人、学校和社区建立关系。结论 识别出需要改善HPV信息传播以解决知识差距和误解。未来的工作可以考虑其他提供者的视角,包括家庭医生和药剂师。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解阿尔伯塔省人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)疫苗接种的障碍和促进因素,因为尽管HPV感染及其相关癌症具有可预防性,但疫苗接种率仍不理想。
设计与对象
这项定性研究涉及对阿尔伯塔省健康区学龄儿童(11-17岁)的父母/监护人进行虚拟访谈,以及对计划相关利益相关者(包括领导者和一线工作人员)进行虚拟焦点小组讨论。
核心方法
本研究采用虚拟访谈和焦点小组的方法,数据分析由健康护理可及性概念框架指导,并使用模板主题分析法进行。
主要结果
HPV疫苗接种的促进因素包括学校为基础的计划的便利性、将疫苗与其他常规疫苗一同推广、与学校、父母/监护人和社区建立关系、包含个人故事的信息传播以及青少年参与。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为强调需要改善HPV信息传播以解决知识差距和误解,这是增强疫苗接种计划的一个新颖见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改善HPV疫苗接种计划提供策略,通过解决知识差距、增强沟通并促进社区参与。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于来自虚拟访谈和焦点小组的定性数据,由于缺乏定量测量和自我报告数据中的潜在偏倚,证据强度中等。

Factors influencing human papillomavirus school-based immunization in Alberta: a qualitative inquiry

Authors: Hannah Sell, Jennifer Malkin, Lisa Allen Scott, Ann M. Toohey, Lisa Allen-Scott
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objectives While human papillomavirus (HPV) infections and associated cancers are preventable through immunization, vaccine coverage for school-aged boys and girls remains suboptimal in Alberta and across Canada. To reconcile this coverage gap and inform Alberta’s school-based HPV immunization program, this study looks to understand barriers and enablers of immunization, how they vary across health zones and population groups, and summarize recommendations to overcome these barriers. Methods Virtual interviews were conducted with members of the Healthier Together Panel of Albertans who were parents/guardians of school-aged children (11–17 years old). Program stakeholders (e.g., leaders, front-line workers) were separately invited to participate in virtual focus groups. Development of study materials and data analysis were guided by the Conceptual Framework of Access to Health Care. Template thematic analysis was used to analyze the interview and focus group data. Results Enablers of HPV immunization included convenience of the school-based program, normalization of the vaccine alongside other routine vaccines, relationship building with schools, parents/guardians, and communities, messaging that included personal stories, and youth engagement. Barriers included gaps in HPV knowledge, perceived lack of trust and transparency, links to sexual activity, consent processes, language barriers, and immunization anxiety. Key recommendations included addressing gaps in HPV messaging, changing the method of program material distribution, employing strategies to reduce immunization anxiety, and continuing to build relationships with parents/guardians, schools, and communities. Conclusion A need to improve HPV messaging to address knowledge gaps and misconceptions was identified. Future work could consider perspectives from other providers, including family physicians and pharmacists.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand barriers and enablers of human papillomavirus (HPV) immunization in Alberta, as vaccine coverage remains suboptimal despite the preventable nature of HPV infections and associated cancers.
Design and population
This qualitative inquiry involved virtual interviews with parents/guardians of school-aged children (11–17 years old) and virtual focus groups with program stakeholders, including leaders and front-line workers, across Alberta's health zones.
Core methods
The study used virtual interviews and focus groups, with data analysis guided by the Conceptual Framework of Access to Health Care and template thematic analysis.
Main results
Enablers of HPV immunization included convenience of the school-based program, normalization of the vaccine alongside other routine vaccines, relationship building with schools, parents/guardians, and communities, messaging that included personal stories, and youth engagement.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the need to improve HPV messaging to address knowledge gaps and misconceptions, which is a novel insight for enhancing immunization programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to improve HPV immunization programs by addressing knowledge gaps, enhancing communication, and fostering community engagement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to qualitative data from virtual interviews and focus groups, and the evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of quantitative measures and potential biases in self-reported data.

拉巴特地区成年女性中人乳头瘤病毒基因型分布:一项回顾性与分析性研究及其对HPV 疫苗接种策略的影响

HUMAN PAPILLOMAVIRUS GENOTYPE DISTRIBUTION AMONG ADULT WOMEN IN THE RABAT REGION (MOROCCO): A RETROSPECTIVE AND ANALYTICAL STUDY WITH IMPLICATIONS FOR HPV VACCINATION STRATEGIES.
作者： Mohamed Rida Tagajdid, Sanae Azelmat, Hamza Goura, Mohamed Rida Znady, Diyae Mansouri, Rachid Abi, Hicham Elannaz, Safae Elkochri, Abdelilah Laraqui, Larbi Bouaiti
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌(CC)仍然是摩洛哥的主要公共卫生挑战,其中它是女性中第二常见的癌症。持续感染高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)是主要的病因因素。
设计与对象
在摩洛哥拉巴特的Mohammed V军事教学医院,于2023年5月至2025年4月期间进行了一项回顾性、描述性和分析性研究。纳入了30至65岁接受常规宫颈癌筛查的无症状女性。
核心方法
使用液基细胞学介质收集宫颈样本,并通过Cobas® 4800检测分析HR-HPV。对于检测出的非HPV16/18的混合HR-HPV阳性样本,采用HPV002®检测进行扩展基因型分析。通过标准化问卷收集社会人口学、行为和临床数据。统计分析包括患病率估计、单变量和多变量逻辑回归,以确定与HR-HPV感染独立相关的因素。
主要结果
Of 2.235 eligible women, HR-HPV prevalence was 5.5% (n=123). HPV16 was the most prevalent genotype (19.5%), followed by HPV68 (12.2%), HPV31 (9.8%), HPV33 (7.3%), HPV35 (7.3%), and HPV39 (7.3%). Single-genotype infections predominated (95%), with dual infections in 5% of cases. Genotypes targeted by the 9-valent HPV vaccine accounted for 58.5% of infections. Multivariate analysis identified diabetes/immunosuppression, hormonal contraceptive use, and inflammatory cervical abnormalities as independent predictors of HR-HPV infection. 75% of HR-HPV-positive women were over 40 years, with a peak prevalence in the 50-59 age group.
解释与创新
本研究首次详细描述了摩洛哥三级医院就诊女性中HR-HPV的患病率和基因型分布。HPV16仍然是主要的基因型。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在摩洛哥扩大HPV 疫苗接种和基于分子的筛查计划的迫切需求,并为监测疫苗的影响和指导公共卫生策略提供了关键的基线数据。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

HUMAN PAPILLOMAVIRUS GENOTYPE DISTRIBUTION AMONG ADULT WOMEN IN THE RABAT REGION (MOROCCO): A RETROSPECTIVE AND ANALYTICAL STUDY WITH IMPLICATIONS FOR HPV VACCINATION STRATEGIES.

Authors: Mohamed Rida Tagajdid, Sanae Azelmat, Hamza Goura, Mohamed Rida Znady, Diyae Mansouri, Rachid Abi, Hicham Elannaz, Safae Elkochri, Abdelilah Laraqui, Larbi Bouaiti
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer (CC) remains a major public health challenge in Morocco, where it is the second most frequent cancer among women. Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) is the primary etiological factor. Limited data exist regarding HPV genotype distribution and associated risk factors in the Moroccan female population. METHODS: A retrospective, descriptive, and analytical study was conducted at the Mohammed V Military Teaching Hospital, Rabat, Morocco, between May 2023 and April 2025. Asymptomatic women aged 30-65 years undergoing routine CC screening were included. Cervical samples were collected using liquid-based cytology medium and analyzed for HR-HPV using the Cobas® 4800 assay. Samples positive for pooled HR-HPV (excluding HPV16/18) underwent extended genotyping using the HPV002® assay. Sociodemographic, behavioral, and clinical data were collected via standardized questionnaires. Statistical analysis included prevalence estimates, univariate, and multivariate logistic regression to identify factors independently associated with HR-HPV infection. RESULTS: Of 2.235 eligible women, HR-HPV prevalence was 5.5% (n=123). HPV16 was the most prevalent genotype (19.5%), followed by HPV68 (12.2%), HPV31 (9.8%), HPV33 (7.3%), HPV35 (7.3%), and HPV39 (7.3%). Single-genotype infections predominated (95%), with dual infections in 5% of cases. Genotypes targeted by the 9-valent HPV vaccine accounted for 58.5% of infections. Multivariate analysis identified diabetes/immunosuppression, hormonal contraceptive use, and inflammatory cervical abnormalities as independent predictors of HR-HPV infection. 75% of HR-HPV-positive women were over 40 years, with a peak prevalence in the 50-59 age group. CONCLUSION: This study provides the first detailed characterization of HR-HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in Moroccan women attending a tertiary hospital. HPV16 remains the predominant genotype. A significant proportion of infections are preventable by the 9-valent vaccine. These findings highlight the urgent need for expanded HPV vaccination and molecular-based screening programs in Morocco and provide a critical baseline for monitoring vaccine impact and guiding public health strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major public health challenge in Morocco, where it is the second most frequent cancer among women. Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) is the primary etiological factor.

This study provides the first detailed characterization of HR-HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in Moroccan women attending a tertiary hospital. HPV16 remains the predominant genotype.

These findings highlight the urgent need for expanded HPV vaccination and molecular-based screening programs in Morocco and provide a critical baseline for monitoring vaccine impact and guiding public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

Polyscias scutellaria叶提取物对药物敏感型和药物耐受型单纯疱疹病毒以及宫颈癌阳性细胞的体外和计算机模拟抗病毒活性:对局部制剂开发的启示。

In vitro and insilico antiviral activity of Polyscias scutellaria leaf extract against drug-susceptible and drug-resistant herpes simplex virus and human papillomavirus-positive cervical cancer: implications for topical formulation development.
作者： Rapeepat Tipayawong, Wiratchanee Kansandee, Chaleampol Loymunkong, Padtaya Sirinantananon, Sirirat Mahawan, Kans Suwanmali, Nattarpart Lookluck, Chamsai Pientong, Tipaya Ekalaksananan, Jureeporn Chuerduangphuia
摘要中文翻译单纯疱疹病毒(HSV)和人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题,特别是在免疫功能低下的人群中。草药提取物已成为开发新型抗病毒药物的有前景候选者,特别是在对抗耐药病毒方面。本研究评估了Polyscias scutellaria(Burm.f.)Fosberg这种热带观赏植物,其嫩芽和嫩叶可食用,对HSV、HPV以及宫颈癌细胞生长的抑制作用。采用乙醇和热水法从新鲜和干燥的叶片中制备的四种提取物,通过斑块抑制、HPV-16假病毒结合、伤口愈合、吖啶/溴化乙锭双染色和流式细胞术检测其对药物敏感型和药物耐受型HSV和HPV的抑制活性,以及对CaSki和HeLa细胞生长的影响。通过定量qPCR和Western blot分析检测基因和蛋白表达水平。新鲜P. scutellaria的乙醇提取物在所有测试提取物中对野生型和药物耐受型HSV株表现出最强的抑制作用,并下调了HSV-1的ICP4、UL30、US6和gC。该提取物还抑制了HPV-16假病毒在早期阶段的感染。此外,它减少了癌细胞增殖并增加了细胞凋亡,同时抑制了HPV-16/18 E6、BCL2和NFKB1的表达,上调了BAX的表达。通过电喷雾离子化-四极杆飞行时间质谱法鉴定出P. scutellaria中具有最高峰面积的潜在代谢物,显示出良好的类药物特性,并通过计算机模拟分析显示出与候选HSV-1和HPV-16蛋白的潜在相互作用。值得注意的是,含有该提取物的原型草本香皂显著降低了耐药型HSV-1感染和HPV-16/18 E6的表达。这些发现突显了P. scutellaria作为有前景的天然抗病毒药物和抗癌剂的潜力,具有开发健康产品的前景。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
单纯疱疹病毒(HSV)和人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题,特别是在免疫功能低下的人群中。
设计与对象
本研究评估了Polyscias scutellaria(Burm.f.)Fosberg这种热带观赏植物,其嫩芽和嫩叶可食用,对HSV、HPV以及宫颈癌细胞生长的抑制作用。
核心方法
采用乙醇和热水法从新鲜和干燥的叶片中制备的四种提取物,通过斑块抑制、HPV-16假病毒结合、伤口愈合、吖啶/溴化乙锭双染色和流式细胞术检测其对药物敏感型和药物耐受型HSV和HPV的抑制活性,以及对CaSki和HeLa细胞生长的影响。
主要结果
新鲜P. scutellaria的乙醇提取物在所有测试提取物中对野生型和药物耐受型HSV株表现出最强的抑制作用,并下调了HSV-1的ICP4、UL30、US6和gC。该提取物还抑制了HPV-16假病毒在早期阶段的感染。此外,它减少了癌细胞增殖并增加了细胞凋亡,同时抑制了HPV-16/18 E6、BCL2和NFKB1的表达,上调了BAX的表达。
解释与创新
通过定量qPCR和Western blot分析检测基因和蛋白表达水平。
科研与公卫意义
通过电喷雾离子化-四极杆飞行时间质谱法鉴定出P. scutellaria中具有最高峰面积的潜在代谢物,显示出良好的类药物特性,并通过计算机模拟分析显示出与候选HSV-1和HPV-16蛋白的潜在相互作用。含有该提取物的原型草本香皂显著降低了耐药型HSV-1感染和HPV-16/18 E6的表达。
局限与证据强度
这些发现突显了P. scutellaria作为有前景的天然抗病毒药物和抗癌剂的潜力,具有开发健康产品的前景。

In vitro and insilico antiviral activity of Polyscias scutellaria leaf extract against drug-susceptible and drug-resistant herpes simplex virus and human papillomavirus-positive cervical cancer: implications for topical formulation development.

Authors: Rapeepat Tipayawong, Wiratchanee Kansandee, Chaleampol Loymunkong, Padtaya Sirinantananon, Sirirat Mahawan, Kans Suwanmali, Nattarpart Lookluck, Chamsai Pientong, Tipaya Ekalaksananan, Jureeporn Chuerduangphuia
Original abstract (English)
Herpes simplex virus (HSV) and human papillomavirus (HPV) infections remain major global health concerns, particularly in immunocompromised individuals. Herbal extracts have emerged as promising candidates for developing novel antiviral agents, particularly against drug-resistant viruses. This study examined the inhibitory effects of Polyscias scutellaria (Burm.f.) Fosberg, a tropical ornamental plant with edible young shoots and leaves against HSV, HPV, and cervical cancer cell growth. Four extracts prepared from fresh and dried leaves using ethanolic and hot-water methods were evaluated for their inhibitory activities against drug-susceptible and drug-resistant HSV and HPV, as well as for their effects on CaSki and HeLa cell growth, using plaque inhibition, HPV-16 pseudovirus binding, wound-healing, acridine/ethidium dual staining, and flow cytometry assays. Gene and protein expression levels were analyzed by quantitative qPCR and western blot analysis. The ethanolic extract of fresh P. scutellaria exhibited the strongest inhibitory effects against both wild-type and drug-resistant HSV strains among all extracts tested and downregulated HSV-1 ICP4, UL30, US6 and gC. This extract also suppressed HPV-16 pseudovirus infection at an early stage. In addition, it reduced cancer cell proliferation and increased apoptosis, while suppressing HPV-16/18 E6, BCL2, and NFKB1 expression and upregulating BAX expression. The putatively annotated metabolites with the highest peak area in P. scutellaria identified using electrospray ionization-quadrupole time-of-flight mass spectrometry, showed favorable drug-likeness and potential interactions with candidate HSV-1 and HPV-16 proteins by in silico analysis. Notably, a prototype herbal soap containing this extract significantly reduced drug-resistant HSV-1 infection and HPV-16/18 E6 expression. These findings highlight P. scutellaria as a promising natural antiviral and anticancer agent with potential for health-product development.

Question and background
Herpes simplex virus (HSV) and human papillomavirus (HPV) infections remain major global health concerns, particularly in immunocompromised individuals.
Design and population

克罗地亚斯普利特大学健康与非健康专业学生对人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种的认知与态度:一项横断面研究

Awareness and attitudes of health and non-health faculty students at the University of Split, Croatia about human papillomavirus vaccination: a cross-sectional study
作者： Ivana Marasović Šušnjara, Marina Ćulav Šolto, Hrvoje Šušnjara, Shelly Melissa Pranić
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨克罗地亚斯普利特大学健康与非健康专业学生对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种的认知、看法和行为,鉴于该地区HPV 疫苗接种率较低。
设计与对象
2024年3月至4月期间,进行了一项横断面调查,有目的地接触了斯普利特大学健康相关和非健康相关专业的学生。
核心方法
该研究使用了一份包含29个二元(是/否)和李克特量表项目的英文调查问卷。采用卡方检验比较频率,并在未调整和调整模型中应用二元逻辑回归分析以探讨相关性。
主要结果
Two hundred and thirty-four (91.4%) health studies students compared to 178 (86.0%) non-health studies students were aware of the HPV vaccine. Less than one-third (n = 123/463, 27%) of the students self-reported being vaccinated against HPV.
解释与创新
作者认为,非健康学生对人乳头瘤病毒作为癌症以及生殖器癌症的病因存在更高的不确定性,并且不愿寻求更多信息与不打算接种疫苗有关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为公共卫生官员提供信息,了解需要针对学生群体采取有针对性的干预措施,特别是那些具备知识但不愿进一步获取更多信息的学生。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和自我报告数据,但由于使用了统计方法,并且社会人口学和行为因素与疫苗接种状态之间存在明确关联,因此证据较强。

Awareness and attitudes of health and non-health faculty students at the University of Split, Croatia about human papillomavirus vaccination: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Ivana Marasović Šušnjara, Marina Ćulav Šolto, Hrvoje Šušnjara, Shelly Melissa Pranić
Original abstract (English)
Awareness, vaccine-related behavior, and perceptions about the human papillomavirus (HPV) vary in certain higher education student populations in Croatia in the context of low HPV vaccination rates. These factors concerning HPV and vaccination in higher education students in diverse fields would inform public health officials about interventions to improve non-vaccination. We aimed to evaluate HPV and HPV vaccine knowledge, perceptions, and behavior of health and non-health students in Split, Croatia. In a cross-sectional survey, we purposively approached students at the University of Split enrolled in health-related and non-health-related programs from March to April 2024. We used a survey with 29 dichotomous (yes/no) and Likert-scale items in English. The survey requested sociodemographic characteristics (12 items), HPV awareness (8 items), and perceptions and attitude (4 items) and behavioral intentions toward HPV vaccination (3 items). We used Chi-square test to compare frequencies and binomial logistic regression in unadjusted and adjusted models to explore associations of sociodemographic variables (unadjusted models only) and knowledge, perception, and behavior variables, HPV vaccination status, intention to be vaccinated, and study program. Of the 463 students who participated in the survey, 256 (55%) were health studies students. Of these, 361 (78%) were female and 287 (62%) were first-year students. Two hundred and thirty-four (91.4%) health studies students compared to 178 (86.0%) non-health studies students were aware of the HPV vaccine, while high proportions of health (98.4%) and non-health (95.7%) students heard about HPV. Non-health studies students reported higher uncertainty about HPV as a cause of cancer (odds ratio [OR] 2.29, 95% CI 1.17–4.47) and anogenital cancer (4.03, 95% CI 2.17–7.50). Less than one-third ( n = 123/463, 27%) of the students self-reported being vaccinated against HPV. Not wanting to seek more information about the vaccine (OR 0.10, 95% CI 0.05–0.21) was associated with not intending to get vaccinated, similarly not knowing family or friends’ vaccination status was associated with self-reported non-vaccination (OR 0.26, 95% CI 0.16–0.42) and not intending to get vaccinated (OR 0.17, 95% CI 0.09–0.34). Although most students were not vaccinated, many students’ awareness was high about HPV and the HPV vaccine. To increase the vaccination rate in similar student populations, public health interventions could target knowledgeable students who may be reluctant to seek more information about the vaccine with early evidence-based interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge, perceptions, and behaviors of health and non-health faculty students in Croatia regarding human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination, given the low vaccination rates in the region.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted from March to April 2024, purposively approaching students at the University of Split enrolled in health-related and non-health-related programs.
Core methods
The study used a survey with 29 dichotomous (yes/no) and Likert-scale items in English. Chi-square tests were used to compare frequencies, and binomial logistic regression was applied in unadjusted and adjusted models to explore associations.
Main results
Two hundred and thirty-four (91.4%) health studies students compared to 178 (86.0%) non-health studies students were aware of the HPV vaccine. Less than one-third (n = 123/463, 27%) of the students self-reported being vaccinated against HPV.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that non-health students had higher uncertainty about HPV as a cause of cancer and anogenital cancer, and that not wanting to seek more information was associated with not intending to get vaccinated.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health officials about the need for targeted interventions to improve HPV vaccination rates among students, particularly those who are knowledgeable but reluctant to seek more information.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and self-reported data, but the evidence is strong due to the use of statistical methods and the clear association of sociodemographic and behavioral factors with vaccination status.

基于污水的监测对人乳头瘤病毒基因型多样性的研究

Wastewater-Based Surveillance of Human Papillomavirus Genotype Diversity
作者： Pauline Michon, Nicolas Cluzel, Yvon Maday, Jean-Luc Prétet, Sabrina Guérin, Rose Lecat, Thomas Thiebault, Vincent Maréchal, Thomas Thiébault
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目的 评估基于污水的流行病学是否能够监测HPV基因型在人群层面的多样性及其时间动态变化。
设计与对象
设计、设置和参与者 本研究为横断面研究,收集了2024年1月24日至2025年1月29日期间,从法国巴黎大区一个服务约100万人口当量的大型污水治疗处理厂中每周两次采集的原始污水样本。
核心方法
人乳头瘤病毒属的HPVs通过广谱定量聚合酶链反应检测,并通过与人类相关的排泄标志物(crAssphage和线粒体DNA)进行标准化。
主要结果
结果 所有分析的100个污水样本中均检测到HPVs,平均浓度为7.3 × 10^3 基因组当量/升(范围,7.3 × 10^1至3.0 × 10^5 基因组当量/升),在整个研究期间表现出中等程度的时间变化。
解释与创新
High-risk HPV-39 and HPV-56, which are not covered by current vaccines, were detected in 72% (95% CI, 63%-81%) and 58% (95% CI, 48%-68%) of samples, respectively, vs 21% (95% CI, 13%-29%) for HPV-16 and 5% (95% CI, 1%-10%) for HPV-18, both of which are vaccine targeted.
科研与公卫意义
目前基于人群层面的监测对循环HPV基因型多样性的监测仍有限,因为临床监测主要依赖宫颈筛查,无法捕捉男性或未参与筛查计划人群的感染情况。
局限与证据强度
The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV-66 (97%; 95% CI, 93%-100%), HPV-6 (93%; 95% CI, 88%-97%), and HPV-53 (89%; 95% CI, 83%-95%).

Wastewater-Based Surveillance of Human Papillomavirus Genotype Diversity

Authors: Pauline Michon, Nicolas Cluzel, Yvon Maday, Jean-Luc Prétet, Sabrina Guérin, Rose Lecat, Thomas Thiebault, Vincent Maréchal, Thomas Thiébault
Original abstract (English)
Importance Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) account for more than 30% of infection-related cancers worldwide. Surveillance of circulating HPV genotypes at the population level remains limited because clinical monitoring primarily relies on cervical screening and does not capture infections in men or individuals outside screening programs. Objective To assess whether wastewater-based epidemiology can monitor the diversity and temporal circulation of HPV genotypes at the population level. Design, Setting, and Participants This is a cross-sectional study of raw wastewater collected twice weekly from January 24, 2024, to January 29, 2025, at a large wastewater treatment plant serving approximately 1 million population equivalents in the Paris, France, metropolitan area. Exposure Population-level shedding of HPV DNA into wastewater. Main Outcomes and Measures The primary outcomes were detection frequency, diversity, and temporal patterns of HPV genotypes in wastewater. HPVs of the genus Alphapapillomavirus were quantified using a broad-spectrum quantitative polymerase chain reaction assay and normalized using human-associated excretion markers (crAssphage and mitochondrial DNA). Twenty-eight HPV genotypes were screened using a multiplex polymerase chain reaction assay. Detection reproducibility was evaluated with McNemar tests (mid-P method); quantification reproducibility was evaluated using Wilcoxon signed-rank tests for censored data. Results HPVs were detected in all 100 wastewater samples analyzed, with a mean concentration of 7.3 × 10 3 genome equivalents/L (range, 7.3 × 10 1 to 3.0 × 10 5 genome equivalents/L), showing moderate temporal variability over the study period. Twenty-seven HPV genotypes were identified, with a median of 6 genotypes per sample (range, 1-12). The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV-66 (97%; 95% CI, 93%-100%), HPV-6 (93%; 95% CI, 88%-97%), and HPV-53 (89%; 95% CI, 83%-95%). High-risk HPV-39 and HPV-56, which are not covered by current vaccines, were detected in 72% (95% CI, 63%-81%) and 58% (95% CI, 48%-68%) of samples, respectively, vs 21% (95% CI, 13%-29%) for HPV-16 and 5% (95% CI, 1%-10%) for HPV-18, both of which are vaccine targeted. Temporal modeling showed relatively stable viral circulation over the study period. Conclusions and Relevance In this cross-sectional study of wastewater collected from a large urban population, wastewater-based epidemiology enabled population-level monitoring of HPV genotype circulation and complemented clinical surveillance by capturing infections across sexes and age groups. These findings suggested that this approach may facilitate evaluation of the effectiveness of HPV vaccination programs and serve as a scalable tool for monitoring HPV genotype diversity within the population.
