A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

9-17岁儿童中第一种俄罗斯四价(重组、吸附)人乳头瘤病毒(6、11、16、18型)疫苗的安全性和免疫原性:多中心、随机、双盲、III期临床试验

Safety and Immunogenicity of First Russian Quadrivalent (Recombinant, Adsorbed) Human Papillomavirus (Types 6, 11, 16, 18) Vaccine in Children Aged 9–17 Years: Multicenter, Randomized, Double-Blind, Phase III Clinical Trial
作者： И В Фельдблюм, Kseniya A. Subbotina, Elena V. Uvarova, Н.А. Пекарева, Ekaterina I. Alekseeva, Р. В. Полибин, Viktoriya S. Yudina, Anna N. Galustyan, Виктор Васильевич Романенко, Svetlana O. Golodnova
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染是与HPV相关的癌前病变和恶性疾病发生的重要风险因素。在俄罗斯联邦开发了重组吸附型疫苗疫苗,以预防这些疾病。疫苗在成人中的安全性和免疫原性已得到研究。本研究旨在评估俄罗斯重组吸附型四价疫苗疫苗在9-17岁儿童中针对HPV(6、11、16、18型)的安全性和免疫原性,并与参考四价疫苗疫苗进行比较。该研究是一项多中心、随机、双盲、平行组、III期临床试验,采用1:1的受试者分配比例。研究纳入了9-17岁健康的HPV血清阴性儿童。9-13岁的受试者接受了试验用疫苗或参考疫苗。从2024年4月2日至2025年4月14日,对所有至少接受一次疫苗疫苗的受试者(按治疗人群)评估了疫苗的安全性。记录了所有与疫苗接种疫苗相关的反应和不良事件(AE),并评估了其与疫苗接种疫苗的相关性、严重程度和持续时间。主要疗效终点是完成全疗程疫苗接种疫苗后1个月针对HPV的抗体几何平均滴度比值(GMT Ratio)(符合方案人群);此外,每种HPV类型的GMT Ratio也被评估。随机化通过计算机生成的列表进行,并通过依次编号、不透明、密封的信封方法确保了分配遮蔽。在研究期间共注册了705例不良事件(AE),其中426例被认为与疫苗接种疫苗相关。未报告严重的疫苗接种相关AE。试验用疫苗在9-13岁和14-17岁两个年龄段中,针对所有四种HPV类型均表现出与参考药物相当的免疫原性。在符合方案人群中,血清转换率范围为97%至100%。9-13岁组的GMT值高于14-17岁组。在两种年龄组中,针对HPV 18型,试验用药物组的GMT值均高于参考药物组。与参考疫苗相比,试验用疫苗在9-17岁儿童中表现出良好的安全性和足够的免疫原性。获得的数据表明,试验用疫苗可能是预防HPV相关疾病的一种有前景的药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染是与HPV相关的癌前病变和恶性疾病发生的重要风险因素。本研究旨在评估一种俄罗斯四价HPV 疫苗疫苗在9-17岁儿童中的安全性和免疫原性。
设计与对象
本研究是一项多中心、随机、双盲、平行组、III期临床试验,采用1:1的受试者分配比例。研究纳入了9-17岁健康的HPV血清阴性儿童。9-13岁的受试者接受了试验用疫苗或参考疫苗。
核心方法
从2024年4月2日至2025年4月14日,对所有至少接受一次疫苗疫苗的受试者(按治疗人群)评估了疫苗疫苗的安全性。主要疗效终点是完成全疗程疫苗接种疫苗后1个月针对HPV的抗体几何平均滴度比值(GMT Ratio)(符合方案人群);此外,每种HPV类型的GMT Ratio也被评估。
主要结果
在研究期间共注册了705例不良事件(AE),其中426例被认为与疫苗接种疫苗相关。未报告严重的疫苗接种相关AE。试验用疫苗在9-13岁和14-17岁两个年龄段中,针对所有四种HPV类型均表现出与参考药物相当的免疫原性。
解释与创新
与参考疫苗疫苗相比,研究中的疫苗在9至17岁儿童中表现出良好的安全性及充分的免疫原性。该研究提供了证据,表明疫苗在两个年龄组中对所有四种HPV类型均具有免疫原性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,试验用疫苗可能是预防HPV相关疾病的一种可行选择。其相当的免疫原性和良好的安全性支持其在针对HPV预防的公共卫生项目中的潜在应用。
局限与证据强度
本研究是在特定的健康HPV血清阴性儿童人群中进行的,这可能限制其结果的普遍适用性。证据强度较高,基于随机、双盲设计以及对安全性和免疫原性终点的评估。

Safety and Immunogenicity of First Russian Quadrivalent (Recombinant, Adsorbed) Human Papillomavirus (Types 6, 11, 16, 18) Vaccine in Children Aged 9–17 Years: Multicenter, Randomized, Double-Blind, Phase III Clinical Trial

Authors: И В Фельдблюм, Kseniya A. Subbotina, Elena V. Uvarova, Н.А. Пекарева, Ekaterina I. Alekseeva, Р. В. Полибин, Viktoriya S. Yudina, Anna N. Galustyan, Виктор Васильевич Романенко, Svetlana O. Golodnova
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a significant risk factor for the development of HPV-associated precancerous and malignant diseases. Recombinant adsorbed vaccine against HPV (types 6, 11, 16, and 18) has been developed in Russian Federation to prevent these conditions. Vaccine immunogenicity and safety have been studied in adults. The aim of the study is to evaluate the safety and immunogenicity of Russian recombinant adsorbed quadrivalent vaccine against HPV (types 6, 11, 16, and 18) in children aged 9–17 years in comparison to reference quadrivalent vaccine. Multicenter, randomized, double-blind, parallel-group, phase III clinical trial with a 1 : 1 participant allocation ratio. The study included healthy seronegative children aged 9–17 years. Study participants aged 9–13 years received either the investigational vaccine or the reference vaccine. Vaccine safety was assessed from April 2, 2024 to April 14, 2025, in all participants who received at least one vaccine dose (as-treated population). Any post-vaccination reactions and adverse events (AE) were recorded, their correlations with vaccination, severity, and duration were evaluated. The primary efficacy endpoint was the geometric mean titer ratio (GMT Ratio) of antibodies to HPV 1 month after full vaccination course (Per-Protocol (PP) population); additionally, the GMT Ratio was evaluated for every HPV type. Randomization was centralized and based on computer-generated list. Allocation concealment was ensured via the method of sequentially numbered, opaque, sealed envelopes. A total of 705 AEs were registered during the study period, 426 of which were considered related to vaccination. No severe vaccination-related AEs were reported. The investigational vaccine demonstrated comparable immunogenicity to the reference drug across all four HPV types in both age cohorts. The seroconversion rate in PP population ranged from 97% to 100%. Higher GMT values were observed in participants aged 9–13 years compared to those aged 14–17 years. GMT values were higher in the investigational drug group in both age cohorts for HPV type 18. Favorable safety profile and adequate immunogenicity of the investigational vaccine were recorded compared to the reference vaccine in children aged 9–17 years. The obtained data suggest that the investigational vaccine is a promising drug for HPV-associated diseases prevention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a significant risk factor for the development of HPV-associated precancerous and malignant diseases. The study aimed to address the need for evaluating the safety and immunogenicity of a Russian quadrivalent HPV vaccine in children aged 9–17 years.
Design and population
A multicenter, randomized, double-blind, parallel-group, phase III clinical trial with a 1 : 1 participant allocation ratio was conducted. The study included healthy seronegative children aged 9–17 years. Participants aged 9–13 years received either the investigational vaccine or the reference vaccine.
Core methods
Vaccine safety was assessed from April 2, 2024, to April 14, 2025, in all participants who received at least one vaccine dose (as-treated population). The primary efficacy endpoint was the geometric mean titer ratio (GMT Ratio) of antibodies to HPV 1 month after full vaccination course (Per-Protocol (PP) population); additionally, the GMT Ratio was evaluated for every HPV type.
Main results
A total of 705 adverse events (AEs) were registered during the study period, 426 of which were considered related to vaccination. No severe vaccination-related AEs were reported. The investigational vaccine demonstrated comparable immunogenicity to the reference drug across all four HPV types in both age cohorts.
Interpretation and novelty
The investigational vaccine showed a favorable safety profile and adequate immunogenicity compared to the reference vaccine in children aged 9–17 years. The study provides evidence that the vaccine is immunogenic across all four HPV types in both age groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the investigational vaccine could be a viable option for preventing HPV-associated diseases in children. The comparable immunogenicity and favorable safety profile support its potential use in public health programs targeting HPV prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a specific population of healthy seronegative children, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is strong, with a high confidence assessment based on the randomized, double-blind design and the evaluation of both safety and immunogenicity endpoints.

一项独特举措:在印度阿萨姆邦为女孩开展的人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种计划

A unique initiative: Human papillomavirus vaccination for girls in Assam, India
作者： Gayatri Gogoi, Dippy Aggarwal, Amay Kashyap Deka
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过为9岁及以上女孩开展独特的疫苗接种计划,解决印度阿萨姆邦缺乏HPV 疫苗接种项目的现状,以预防宫颈癌并提高对HPV相关疾病的认识。
设计与对象
本研究是一项在印度阿萨姆邦开展的疫苗接种计划,时间为2025年3月至2026年2月,针对9岁及以上女孩。总计有2000名女孩接受了疫苗疫苗,共接种了3800剂。
核心方法
该计划涉及与一家非营利组织合作,为9岁及以上女孩接种印度本土研发的HPV 疫苗疫苗“CERVAVAC”。研究重点是提高目标人群女孩对HPV相关疾病的意识并推广疫苗接种。
主要结果
调查结果显示,阿萨姆邦不到1%的女孩曾接种过HPV疫苗,而精英学术机构的学生中仅有39%有相关意识,这归因于较低的社会经济地位、人口异质性以及根深蒂固的社会文化观念。
解释与创新
本研究表明,社会文化和经济因素显著影响了HPV 疫苗接种的覆盖率和意识水平,强调了在资源匮乏地区开展针对性意识宣传活动的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了应对社会文化障碍以提高HPV 疫苗接种接种率和宫颈癌预防措施的重要性,特别是在医疗资源有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于仅针对一个州,并且基于调查数据而非临床结果。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它提供了关于印度阿萨姆邦HPV 疫苗接种接种挑战的见解。

A unique initiative: Human papillomavirus vaccination for girls in Assam, India

Authors: Gayatri Gogoi, Dippy Aggarwal, Amay Kashyap Deka
Original abstract (English)
Abstract: The first human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination initiative was undertaken in Assam, with an indigenously made vaccine in India, “CERVAVAC,” in association with a nonprofit organization during March 2025–February 2026. The main objective was to initiate HPV vaccination among girls from 9 years and above to create widespread awareness and prevent cervical cancer. A total of 2000 girls received the vaccine with a total of 3800 doses. Persistent infection with high-risk HPV genotypes is the principal etiological factor for cervical cancer, and prophylactic vaccination can prevent up to 90% of cases. A survey in Assam showed less that 1% had previously vaccinated against HPV, and awareness was 39% among students of elite academic institutions due to lower socioeconomic status, population heterogeneity, and deeply rooted sociocultural beliefs significantly contributing to less screening and vaccination coverage. Nongovernmental organizations played a pivotal role in spreading awareness and vaccination all over India, but this vaccination drive was the first initiative in the state of Assam. The impact was an infinite transformation of mindset to a finite measurable outcome, setting a national record of highest vaccination by a nongovernmental organization within 1 year time.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of HPV vaccination programs in Assam, India, by initiating a unique initiative to vaccinate girls aged 9 years and above to prevent cervical cancer and raise awareness about HPV-related diseases.
Design and population
The study was a vaccination initiative conducted in Assam, India, from March 2025 to February 2026, targeting girls aged 9 years and above. A total of 2000 girls received the vaccine, with 3800 doses administered.
Core methods
The initiative involved administering an indigenously made HPV vaccine, CERVAVAC, in collaboration with a nonprofit organization. The study focused on raising awareness and promoting vaccination among girls in the target population.
Main results
A survey revealed that less than 1% of girls in Assam had previously been vaccinated against HPV, and awareness was 39% among students of elite academic institutions due to factors such as lower socioeconomic status, population heterogeneity, and sociocultural beliefs.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that sociocultural and economic factors significantly influenced HPV vaccination coverage and awareness, highlighting the need for targeted awareness campaigns in low-resource settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of addressing sociocultural barriers to improve HPV vaccination rates and cervical cancer prevention in regions with limited healthcare access.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single state and the use of survey data rather than clinical outcomes. However, the evidence is strong as it provides insights into the challenges of HPV vaccination in Assam, India.

患病率和人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染在福建省漳州市医院接受人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)检测的女性中的基因型分布:一项回顾性研究

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infection among women undergoing HPV testing at a hospital in Zhangzhou, China: a retrospective study
作者： Wenxin Xu, Yueli Guo, Weide Chen, Guowei Wang, Qingyu Wu, Yuanjun Zeng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析福建省漳州市某医院接受人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)检测的女性中患病率和人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的基因型分布,以提供该人群人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)流行病学模式的见解。
设计与对象
本研究是一项回顾性、单中心研究,对象为福建省漳州市某医院体检中心接受HPV检测的13,492名年龄在18至85岁之间的女性。
核心方法
通过低密度基因芯片和流杂交技术使用HPV分型检测试剂盒收集宫颈脱落细胞进行HPV检测。进行了二元逻辑回归分析以评估HPV 患病率与年龄之间的关系。
主要结果
共检测到27种HPV类型,包括17种高危型HPV(高危型HPV)和10种低风险/不确定致癌意义的HPV(LR-HPV)。总体HPV感染率为16.86%(2,275/13,492),其中12.59%(1,699/13,492)为单一HPV型感染,4.27%(576/13,492)为多重HPV型感染。HPV的患病率表现出与年龄相关的“双峰”分布,峰值出现在≤29岁和≥60岁年龄组。
解释与创新
本研究揭示了福建省漳州市女性中HPV感染的基本基因型和年龄特异性分布,强调了针对最常见HPV类型的监测和疫苗接种意识的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为HPV预防干预措施的评估提供了有价值的见解,并强调了在该人群中定期进行HPV 监测的重要性,特别是对于高流行HPV类型。
局限与证据强度
本研究是回顾性的,并基于单中心样本,这可能限制其推广性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它基于对大量队列研究的直接HPV检测和统计分析。

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infection among women undergoing HPV testing at a hospital in Zhangzhou, China: a retrospective study

Authors: Wenxin Xu, Yueli Guo, Weide Chen, Guowei Wang, Qingyu Wu, Yuanjun Zeng
Original abstract (English)
Introduction This retrospective, single-center study aims to analyze the prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in women undergoing HPV testing at a hospital in Zhangzhou, China. Methods A total of 13,492 females, 18–85 years, undergoing HPV testing at the physical examination center of a hospital in Zhangzhou, China, were recruited. Cervical cast-off cells were collected for HPV testing by low-density gene chip and flow hybridization technology using HPV typing test kit. A total of 27 kinds of HPV types were detected using this method, including 17 kinds of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) and 10 kinds of low-risk/uncertain oncogenic significance HPV (LR-HPV). Results The overall HPV infection rate was 16.86% (2,275/13,492), with 12.59% (1,699/13,492) females detected as single HPV-type infection and 4.27% (576/13492) females detected as multiple HPV-type infection. Among all 27 detected HPV types, HPV52 exhibited the highest prevalence (3.58%, 483/13,492), followed by HPV53 (1.76%, 237/13,492) and HPV81 (1.71%, 231/13,492). The most common HPV coinfection pattern was HPV52 + HPV53 (12/2,275). Overall HPV prevalence showed a “two-peak” age-related distribution, with the first peak in the ≤29-year age group (19.69%) and the second peak in the ≥60-year age group (24.45%), respectively. It decreased in the 40- to 49-year age group (13.64%). The prevalence of single/multiple HPV and HR-HPV/LR-HPV infection also exhibited age-related trends similar to those described above. Binary logistic regression analysis revealed a non-linear relationship between HPV prevalence and age ( p < 0.05). Conclusion Our study revealed the basic genotype- and age-specific distribution of HPV infection among females attending the physical examination center of a hospital in Zhangzhou. These results emphasized that the specific population should strengthen regular HPV surveillance and improve awareness of vaccination, especially for the highly prevalent HPV types (HPV52,53,51). In addition, our findings provide valuable insights for the measurement of HPV prevention interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to analyze the prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in women undergoing HPV testing at a hospital in Zhangzhou, China, to provide insights into the epidemiological patterns of HPV in this population.
Design and population
A retrospective, single-center study was conducted on 13,492 females aged 18–85 years who underwent HPV testing at the physical examination center of a hospital in Zhangzhou, China.
Core methods
Cervical cast-off cells were collected for HPV testing using low-density gene chip and flow hybridization technology. Binary logistic regression analysis was performed to assess the relationship between HPV prevalence and age.
Main results
A total of 27 HPV types were detected, including 17 high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) and 10 low-risk/uncertain oncogenic significance HPV (LR-HPV). The overall HPV infection rate was 16.86% (2,275/13,492), with 12.59% (1,699/13,492) being single HPV-type infections and 4.27% (576/13,492) being multiple HPV-type infections. The prevalence of HPV showed a "two-peak" age-related distribution, with peaks in the ≤29-year and ≥60-year age groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The study revealed the basic genotype- and age-specific distribution of HPV infection among females in Zhangzhou, highlighting the need for targeted surveillance and awareness of vaccination for the most prevalent HPV types.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide valuable insights for the measurement of HPV prevention interventions and emphasize the importance of regular HPV surveillance in this population, particularly for the highly prevalent HPV types.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on a single-center sample, which may limit generalizability. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on direct HPV testing and statistical analysis of a large cohort.

父母对在尼日利亚伊莱-伊费为9-14岁女性儿童接种人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗疫苗的看法和意愿:计划行为理论方法

Parental perception and intention to vaccinate female children 9–14 years with the human papillomavirus vaccine in Ile-Ife, Nigeria: theory of planned behaviour approach
作者： Olorunfemi Akinbode Ogundele, Funmito Omolola Fehintola, Olaoti Oyeyemi Ogundare, Adeola Margret Adejuwon, Evelyn Temidayo Atoyebi, Kizito Chidera Igweshi, Daniel Ayobami Ogunleye, Deborah Ayomikun Solomon, Oluwatobi Francis Tunwase, Chidiebele George Umeadi
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)疫苗接种是预防宫颈癌的一种高度有效的策略;然而,实现对符合条件的女孩的高疫苗接种覆盖率在很大程度上取决于父母的决策。本研究采用计划行为理论(TPB)评估了在尼日利亚伊莱-伊费为9-14岁女性儿童接种HPV疫苗的父母看法和意愿。2024年8月至9月期间,采用多阶段抽样技术,在尼日利亚伊莱-伊费的163名9-14岁女性儿童的父母中进行了一项横断面研究。数据通过预先测试的问卷收集,该问卷基于先前验证的工具开发,并使用IBM SPSS Statistics版本27进行分析。进行了卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归分析,以确定与父母接种意愿相关因素,显著性水平为p < 0.05。共发放了163份问卷,其中160份(98.2%)被完成并分析(回复率:98.2%)。总体而言,59.4%的父母对HPV 疫苗接种有积极看法,而60.6%的父母表达了为女性儿童接种疫苗的意愿。较高的教育水平(调整后比值比[AOR] = 6.06;95%置信区间[CI] 2.11–17.38)和感知行为控制(AOR = 11.97;95% CI 4.69–30.56)与父母接种意愿相关。总之,父母对HPV 疫苗接种的积极看法和为女性儿童接种疫苗的意愿均不理想。感知行为控制和父母的教育水平是父母接种意愿的重要预测因素。因此,旨在加强父母接种意愿的干预措施应关注这些因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在使用计划行为理论(TPB)评估尼日利亚伊莱-伊费为9-14岁女性儿童接种HPV疫苗的父母看法和意愿。科学/公共卫生问题是需要提高符合条件女孩的HPV 疫苗接种覆盖率,因为父母的决策至关重要。
设计与对象
采用多阶段抽样技术,在尼日利亚伊莱-伊费的163名9-14岁女性儿童的父母中进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
数据通过预先测试的问卷收集,并使用IBM SPSS Statistics版本27进行分析,进行了卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归分析。
主要结果
共发放了163份问卷,其中160份(98.2%)被完成。较高的教育水平和感知行为控制与父母接种意愿相关,调整后的比值比分别为6.06和11.97。
解释与创新
研究发现,父母对HPV 疫苗接种的积极看法和为女性儿童接种疫苗的意愿均不理想,感知行为控制和父母的教育水平是意愿的重要预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,旨在加强父母接种意愿的干预措施应关注这些因素,以提高HPV 疫苗接种的覆盖率。
局限与证据强度
本研究样本量有限,回复率也较低,研究结果可能无法推广到其他人群。证据强度为中等。

Parental perception and intention to vaccinate female children 9–14 years with the human papillomavirus vaccine in Ile-Ife, Nigeria: theory of planned behaviour approach

Authors: Olorunfemi Akinbode Ogundele, Funmito Omolola Fehintola, Olaoti Oyeyemi Ogundare, Adeola Margret Adejuwon, Evelyn Temidayo Atoyebi, Kizito Chidera Igweshi, Daniel Ayobami Ogunleye, Deborah Ayomikun Solomon, Oluwatobi Francis Tunwase, Chidiebele George Umeadi
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is a highly effective strategy for preventing cervical cancer; however, achieving high vaccination coverage among eligible girls depends largely on parental decision-making. This study assessed parental perceptions and intention to vaccinate female children aged 9–14 years against HPV in Ile-Ife, Nigeria, using the Theory of Planned Behaviour (TPB). A cross-sectional study was conducted between August and September 2024 among 163 parents of female children aged 9–14 years attending selected secondary schools in Ile-Ife, Nigeria, using a multistage sampling technique. Data were collected using a pretested questionnaire developed from previously validated instruments and analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 27. Chi-square tests and multivariable logistic regression were performed to identify factors associated with parental intention to vaccinate at a significance level of p < 0.05. Of the 163 questionnaires distributed, 160 (98.2%) were completed and analysed (response rate: 98.2%). Overall, 59.4% of parents had a positive perception of HPV vaccination, while 60.6% expressed an intention to vaccinate their female children. Higher education level (adjusted odds ratio [AOR] = 6.06; 95% confidence interval [CI] 2.11–17.38) and perceived behavioural control (AOR = 11.97; 95% CI 4.69–30.56) were associated with parental intention to vaccinate. In conclusion, both positive perception of HPV vaccination and parental intention to vaccinate female children were suboptimal. Perceived behavioural control and parental educational level were significant predictors of parental intention to vaccinate. Interventions aimed at strengthening parental intention should therefore give attention to these factors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess parental perceptions and intention to vaccinate female children against HPV in Ile-Ife, Nigeria, using the Theory of Planned Behaviour (TPB). The scientific/public-health problem is the need to improve HPV vaccination coverage among eligible girls, as parental decision-making is crucial.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 163 parents of female children aged 9–14 years in Ile-Ife, Nigeria, using a multistage sampling technique.
Core methods
Data were collected using a pretested questionnaire and analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 27, with chi-square tests and multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Of the 163 questionnaires distributed, 160 (98.2%) were completed. Higher education level and perceived behavioural control were associated with parental intention to vaccinate, with adjusted odds ratios of 6.06 and 11.97, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that both positive perception of HPV vaccination and parental intention to vaccinate were suboptimal, with perceived behavioural control and parental educational level being significant predictors of intention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that interventions aimed at strengthening parental intention to vaccinate should focus on these factors to improve HPV vaccination coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study has a limited sample size and response rate, and the findings may not be generalizable to other populations. The evidence strength is moderate.

台湾大学生HPV预防健康素养量表的开发与心理测量学验证

Development and Psychometric Validation of the Human Papillomavirus Prevention Health Literacy Scale for College Students in Taiwan.
作者： Ching-Yi Lai, Shu-Chen Yang, Chieh-Yu Liu, Hung-Ru Lin, Yuan-Mei Liao, Tzu-Ying Lee
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一种常见的性传播病毒,与多种癌症有关。由于大学生属于高风险群体,了解他们关于HPV预防的健康素养至关重要。然而,目前尚无适合的测量工具可用于此目的。目的:本研究开发了HPV预防健康素养量表(HPV-HLS),并对其信度和效度进行了评估。方法:采用横断面研究设计,该量表在台湾北部两所大学和中部一所大学的372名大学生样本中进行了验证。初始量表包含49个项目,来源于定性访谈和文献综述。在内容效度评估和项目分析后,量表被精简为37个项目。对150名参与者进行了探索性因子分析(EFA),随后对222名参与者进行了验证性因子分析(CFA)。评估了同时效度和内部一致性信度。数据收集时间为2021年11月至2022年12月。结果:EFA的结果显示,一个双因素模型解释了总变异的60.30%,而CFA的结果表明拟合指标良好(GFI = .81,CFI = .92,TLI = .91,RMSEA = .07,SRMR = .02)。此外,两个因素的平均方差提取(AVE)分别为.56和.64,对应的组合信度(CR)值分别为.96和.93,表明具有良好的收敛效度。值得注意的是,AVE超过了相应因素的平方积矩相关系数,证实了良好的区分效度。最终的HPV-HLS量表包含21个项目,分为两个子量表:一般信息的理解与评估以及从医疗专业人员获取信息。该量表与中文多维健康素养问卷(r = .68,p < .001)之间表现出良好的同时效度。HPV-HLS表现出良好的内部一致性(Cronbach's α = .959)。结论/对未来实践的启示:经过严格的心理测量学测试,新开发的HPV-HLS表现出良好的信度和效度,使其成为评估大学生HPV预防健康素养的客观工具。该HPV-HLS可由医疗保健提供者和教育工作者用于评估健康素养,并评估预防HPV感染的临床干预措施的有效性。

问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一种常见的性传播病毒,与多种癌症有关。由于大学生属于高风险群体,了解他们关于HPV预防的健康素养至关重要。
设计与对象
采用横断面研究设计,该量表在台湾北部两所大学和中部一所大学的372名大学生样本中进行了验证。
核心方法
初始量表包含49个项目,来源于定性访谈和文献综述。在内容效度评估和项目分析后,量表被精简为37个项目。对150名参与者进行了探索性因子分析(EFA),随后对222名参与者进行了验证性因子分析(CFA)。评估了同时效度和内部一致性信度。
主要结果
EFA的结果显示,一个双因素模型解释了总变异的60.30%,而CFA的结果表明拟合指标良好(GFI = .81,CFI = .92,TLI = .91,RMSEA = .07,SRMR = .02)。此外,两个因素的平均方差提取(AVE)分别为.56和.64,对应的组合信度(CR)值分别为.96和.93,表明具有良好的收敛效度。值得注意的是,AVE超过了相应因素的平方积矩相关系数,证实了良好的区分效度。最终的HPV-HLS量表包含21个项目,分为两个子量表:一般信息的理解与评估以及从医疗专业人员获取信息。

局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告该研究的具体局限性。

Development and Psychometric Validation of the Human Papillomavirus Prevention Health Literacy Scale for College Students in Taiwan.

Authors: Ching-Yi Lai, Shu-Chen Yang, Chieh-Yu Liu, Hung-Ru Lin, Yuan-Mei Liao, Tzu-Ying Lee
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted virus associated with several types of cancer. Because college students are a high-risk group, understanding their health literacy with regard to HPV prevention is critical. However, no suitable measurement tools are currently available for this purpose. PURPOSE: In this study, an HPV Prevention Health Literacy Scale (HPV-HLS) was developed, and its reliability and validity were assessed. METHODS: A cross-sectional design was used, and the developed scale was validated on a sample of 372 college students from two universities in northern Taiwan and one university in central Taiwan. The initial scale comprised 49 items, developed from qualitative interviews and a literature review. After content validity assessment and item analysis, the scale was refined to 37 items. Exploratory factor analysis (EFA) was conducted with 150 participants, followed by confirmatory factor analysis (CFA) with 222 participants. Concurrent validity and internal consistency reliability were assessed. Data were collected from November 2021 to December 2022. RESULTS: The results of the EFA revealed a two-factor model accounted for 60.30% of the total variation, and those of the CFA demonstrated favorable goodness-of-fit indices (GFI = .81, CFI = .92, TLI = .91, RMSEA = .07, and SRMR = .02). Moreover, the average variance extracted (AVE) for the two factors were .56 and .64, respectively, with corresponding composite reliability (CR) values of .96 and .93, indicating excellent convergent validity. Notably, the AVE surpassed the squared product-moment correlation of the corresponding factors, affirming good discriminant validity. The final iteration of the HPV-HLS comprised 21 items with two subscales: comprehension and evaluation of general information and seeking information from health care professionals. Concurrent validity was found between the proposed scale and the Mandarin Multidimensional Health Literacy Questionnaire ( r = .68, p < .001). The HPV-HLS demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's α = .959). CONCLUSIONS/IMPLICATIONS FOR FUTURE PRACTICE: After rigorous psychometric testing, the newly developed HPV-HLS demonstrated excellent reliability and validity, establishing it as an objective tool for evaluating HPV prevention health literacy among college students. This HPV-HLS may be used by health care providers and educators to assess health literacy and evaluate the effectiveness of clinical interventions to prevent HPV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted virus associated with several types of cancer. Because college students are a high-risk group, understanding their health literacy with regard to HPV prevention is critical.
Design and population
A cross-sectional design was used, and the developed scale was validated on a sample of 372 college students from two universities in northern Taiwan and one university in central Taiwan.
Core methods
The initial scale comprised 49 items, developed from qualitative interviews and a literature review. After content validity assessment and item analysis, the scale was refined to 37 items. Exploratory factor analysis (EFA) was conducted with 150 participants, followed by confirmatory factor analysis (CFA) with 222 participants. Concurrent validity and internal consistency reliability were assessed.
Main results
The results of the EFA revealed a two-factor model accounted for 60.30% of the total variation, and those of the CFA demonstrated favorable goodness-of-fit indices (GFI = .81, CFI = .92, TLI = .91, RMSEA = .07, and SRMR = .02). Moreover, the average variance extracted (AVE) for the two factors were .56 and .64, respectively, with corresponding composite reliability (CR) values of .96 and .93, indicating excellent convergent validity. Notably, the AVE surpassed the squared product-moment correlation of the corresponding factors, affirming good discriminant validity. The final iteration of the HPV-HLS comprised 21 items with two subscales: comprehension and evaluation of general information and seeking information from health care professionals.
Interpretation and novelty
After rigorous psychometric testing, the newly developed HPV-HLS demonstrated excellent reliability and validity, establishing it as an objective tool for evaluating HPV prevention health literacy among college students.
Scientific and public-health significance
This HPV-HLS may be used by health care providers and educators to assess health literacy and evaluate the effectiveness of clinical interventions to prevent HPV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study.

HPV感染过程中宫颈阴道微生物组的变化

Cervicovaginal microbiome alterations across HPV infection
作者： Laura Chulenbayeva, Aidana Rakhmankulova, Nazira Kamzayeva, Samat Kozhakhmetov, Milan Terzic, Gauri Bapayeva, Gulzhanat Aimagambetova, Yevgeniy Kim, Berik Primbetov, Balkenzhe Imankulova
摘要中文翻译引言 宫颈阴道微生物组被认为是影响HPV相关宫颈疾病的一个潜在调节因素,但其与病毒类型异质性、细胞学分级以及社区状态类型(CST)之间的关系仍不完全明确。方法 本横断面研究利用全基因组宏基因组测序、整合多样性分析、MaAsLin2差异丰度检验以及HUMAnN 3.0功能通路分析,对311名非妊娠女性在HPV感染状态、病毒类型分类(无HPV、高危型HPV、HPV16、HPV18、其他HPV)、宫颈细胞学分级以及CSTs中的宫颈阴道微生物组组成和功能潜力进行了特征分析。结果 HPV阳性样本表现出更高的细菌物种丰富度,并且与HPV阴性样本相比,乳酸杆菌与加德纳菌的主导地位发生了相互转换。其他HPV表现出最高的细菌物种丰富度,并具有独特的分类学和预测功能关联,包括铁载体和脂多糖合成通路的推断丰度较高;然而,总体物种水平的微生物组组成在不同HPV类型组之间没有显著差异。CST I和CST II无论HPV状态如何都表现出组成稳定性,而CST IV在HPV阳性女性中表现出显著的乳酸杆菌减少。LSIL与NILM相比,表现出更低的Fannyhessea vaginae和Alloscardovia omnicolens丰度,以及更高的Phocaeicola vulgatus丰度。讨论 这些发现表明,致癌潜力和微生物失调严重程度并不一致,且CST类型可能调节HPV与微生物组的相互作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨宫颈阴道微生物组与HPV感染之间的关系,特别是病毒类型异质性、细胞学分级以及社区状态类型(CST)如何影响这种关系。
设计与对象
本横断面研究分析了311名非妊娠女性,以特征化HPV感染状态、病毒类型分类、宫颈细胞学分级以及CSTs中的宫颈阴道微生物组组成和功能潜力。
核心方法
本研究采用全基因组宏基因组测序、整合多样性分析、MaAsLin2差异丰度检验以及HUMAnN 3.0功能通路分析,以评估微生物组的组成和功能潜力。
主要结果
HPV阳性样本表现出更高的细菌物种丰富度,并且与HPV阴性样本相比,乳酸杆菌与加德纳菌的主导地位发生了相互转换。其他HPV表现出最高的细菌物种丰富度,并具有独特的分类学和预测功能关联,包括铁载体和脂多糖合成通路的推断丰度较高。CST IV在HPV阳性女性中表现出显著的乳酸杆菌减少。LSIL与NILM相比,表现出更低的Fannyhessea vaginae和Alloscardovia omnicolens丰度,以及更高的Phocaeicola vulgatus丰度。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,致癌潜力和微生物失调严重程度并不一致,且CST类型可能调节HPV与微生物组的相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为理解微生物组在HPV感染中的作用及其对宫颈疾病进展的潜在影响提供了见解,强调了CST在调节HPV-微生物组相互作用中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究为横断面研究,限制了因果推断的可能,且结果基于推断的功能通路而非直接测量,这可能影响证据的强度。

Cervicovaginal microbiome alterations across HPV infection

Authors: Laura Chulenbayeva, Aidana Rakhmankulova, Nazira Kamzayeva, Samat Kozhakhmetov, Milan Terzic, Gauri Bapayeva, Gulzhanat Aimagambetova, Yevgeniy Kim, Berik Primbetov, Balkenzhe Imankulova
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The cervicovaginal microbiome is a proposed modifier of HPV-associated cervical disease, yet its relationship with viral type heterogeneity, cytological grade, and community state type (CST) remains incompletely understood. Methods This cross-sectional study characterized cervicovaginal microbiome composition and functional potential across HPV infection status, viral type categories (No HPV, HPV High Risk, HPV16, HPV18, HPV Other), cervical cytological grades, and CSTs in 311 non-pregnant women using whole-genome shotgun metagenomic sequencing, integrated diversity analyses, MaAsLin2 differential abundance testing, and HUMAnN 3.0 functional pathway profiling. Results HPV-positive samples showed increased bacterial species richness, and a reciprocal Lactobacillus -to- Gardnerella dominance shift compared to HPV-negative samples. HPV Other showed the highest bacterial species richness and distinct taxonomic and predicted functional associations, including higher inferred abundance of siderophore- and lipopolysaccharide-biosynthesis pathways; however, overall species-level community composition did not differ significantly across HPV type groups. CST I and CST II exhibited compositional stability regardless of HPV status, while CST IV showed pronounced Lactobacillus depletion in HPV-positive women. LSIL was associated with lower Fannyhessea vaginae and Alloscardovia omnicolens abundance and higher Phocaeicola vulgatus abundance relative to NILM. Discussion These findings indicate that oncogenic potential and dysbiosis severity are not aligned, and that CST type modifies HPV–microbiome interactions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between the cervicovaginal microbiome and HPV infection, particularly focusing on how viral type heterogeneity, cytological grade, and community state type (CST) might influence this relationship.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study analyzed 311 non-pregnant women to characterize cervicovaginal microbiome composition and functional potential across HPV infection status, viral type categories, cervical cytological grades, and CSTs.
Core methods
The study used whole-genome shotgun metagenomic sequencing, integrated diversity analyses, MaAsLin2 differential abundance testing, and HUMAnN 3.0 functional pathway profiling to assess microbiome composition and functional potential.
Main results
HPV-positive samples showed increased bacterial species richness and a reciprocal Lactobacillus-to-Gardnerella dominance shift compared to HPV-negative samples. HPV Other had the highest bacterial species richness and distinct taxonomic and predicted functional associations, including higher inferred abundance of siderophore- and lipopolysaccharide-biosynthesis pathways. CST IV showed pronounced Lactobacillus depletion in HPV-positive women. LSIL was associated with lower Fannyhessea vaginae and Alloscardovia omnicolens abundance and higher Phocaeicola vulgatus abundance relative to NILM.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that oncogenic potential and dysbiosis severity are not aligned, and that CST type modifies HPV–microbiome interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the microbiome's role in HPV infection and its potential implications for cervical disease progression, highlighting the importance of CST in modifying HPV–microbiome interactions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, and the results are based on inferred functional pathways rather than direct measurements, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

Journal: Journal of Clinical Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-23  |  DOI: 10.3390/jcm15197386  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 15, 19, 7386
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

有高危型HPV阳性史女性在监测、阴道镜检查和LEEP治疗过程中性功能及心理压力的差异:一项横断面研究

Sexual Function and Psychological Distress Across Surveillance, Colposcopy, and LEEP in Women with a History of High-Risk HPV Positivity: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Başak Özge Kayan, Gokce Nur Esen Topal
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨在有高危型HPV阳性史的女性中,宫颈管理的各个阶段是否对性功能和心理压力负担产生差异。
设计与对象
本研究为单中心横断面研究,纳入了302名有高危型HPV阳性史的育龄女性,分为三组:监测组(n = 50)、阴道镜检查组(n = 177)和环形电切术(LEEP)组(n = 75)。
核心方法
参与者完成了女性性功能指数(FSFI)、WHOQOL-BREF量表和医院焦虑抑郁量表(HADS)。使用Kruskal–Wallis检验和卡方检验比较各组;FSFI总分通过多变量线性回归建模,FSFI定义的功能障碍通过逻辑回归分析。
主要结果
No statistically significant between-group differences were observed in FSFI total, subdomains, HADS, or WHOQOL domains. FSFI-defined dysfunction was frequent across groups, with no statistically significant difference (59–67%). Depressive symptoms were associated with FSFI-defined dysfunction (OR = 1.14, 95% CI 1.03–1.26, p = 0.009), but not with continuous FSFI (B = −0.37, p = 0.064). Marital status was the strongest overall correlate.
解释与创新
作者认为,管理组之间的性功能无显著差异,而在探索性分析中,抑郁症状与性功能障碍表现出最一致的关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,在有高危型HPV阳性史的女性心理性健康护理中,应考虑心理社会和关系因素,强调了对筛查和咨询策略进行进一步研究的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究为横断面研究,限制了因果推论的可能,且调整模型中未发现管理路径与FSFI之间的显著关联,表明证据强度中等。

Sexual Function and Psychological Distress Across Surveillance, Colposcopy, and LEEP in Women with a History of High-Risk HPV Positivity: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Başak Özge Kayan, Gokce Nur Esen Topal
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) positivity and its management may affect sexual function and psychological well-being, yet whether this burden differs across the successive steps of cervical management is unclear. We compared sexual function, quality of life, and psychological distress across three management steps—surveillance, colposcopy, and loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP). Methods: In this single-center cross-sectional study, 302 reproductive-age women with a history of hrHPV positivity (surveillance, n = 50; colposcopy, n = 177; LEEP, n = 75) completed the Female Sexual Function Index (FSFI), WHOQOL-BREF, and Hospital Anxiety and Depression Scale (HADS). Groups were compared with Kruskal–Wallis and chi-square tests; FSFI total was modelled by multivariable linear regression and FSFI-defined dysfunction by logistic regression. Results: No statistically significant between-group differences were observed in FSFI total (medians 23.8/25.2/24.5; p = 0.897), FSFI subdomains, HADS, or WHOQOL domains; FSFI-defined dysfunction was frequent across groups, with no statistically significant between-group difference (59–67%). No statistically significant association between management pathway and FSFI was observed in the adjusted models (LEEP vs. surveillance B = −0.63, 95% CI −4.68 to +3.42). Depressive symptoms—but not anxiety—were associated with FSFI-defined dysfunction (per HADS-D point, OR = 1.14, 95% CI 1.03–1.26, p = 0.009), whereas the association with continuous FSFI was not significant (B = −0.37, p = 0.064). Marital status was the strongest overall correlate. Conclusions: Across surveillance, colposcopy, and LEEP, sexual function did not differ significantly between management groups at the time of assessment, and, in exploratory analyses, depressive symptoms showed the most consistent association with sexual dysfunction. These findings highlight the potential relevance of psychosocial and relational context in the psychosexual care of women with a history of hrHPV positivity, although prospective studies are needed before specific screening or counselling recommendations can be made.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether the burden of sexual function and psychological distress differs across the successive steps of cervical management in women with a history of high-risk HPV positivity.
Design and population
A single-center cross-sectional study was conducted involving 302 reproductive-age women with a history of hrHPV positivity, divided into three groups: surveillance (n = 50), colposcopy (n = 177), and loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) (n = 75).
Core methods
Participants completed the Female Sexual Function Index (FSFI), WHOQOL-BREF, and Hospital Anxiety and Depression Scale (HADS). Groups were compared using Kruskal–Wallis and chi-square tests, while FSFI total was modelled by multivariable linear regression and FSFI-defined dysfunction by logistic regression.
Main results
No statistically significant between-group differences were observed in FSFI total, subdomains, HADS, or WHOQOL domains. FSFI-defined dysfunction was frequent across groups, with no statistically significant difference (59–67%). Depressive symptoms were associated with FSFI-defined dysfunction (OR = 1.14, 95% CI 1.03–1.26, p = 0.009), but not with continuous FSFI (B = −0.37, p = 0.064). Marital status was the strongest overall correlate.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that sexual function did not differ significantly between management groups, and in exploratory analyses, depressive symptoms showed the most consistent association with sexual dysfunction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the importance of considering psychosocial and relational factors in the psychosexual care of women with a history of hrHPV positivity, highlighting the need for further research into screening and counseling strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, and the adjusted models showed no significant association between management pathway and FSFI, suggesting the evidence strength is moderate.

AI聊天机器人用于日本HPV疫苗接种知识的提升

AI Chatbot Use for Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Literacy in Japan
作者： Kana Kobayashi, Zhengdong Wu, Hana Tomoi, Ken Masuda, Heidi J. Larson, Kathy Leung, Joseph T. Wu, Leesa Lin
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过评估AI驱动的HPV疫苗接种聊天机器人与政府传单相比的效果,解决日本HPV疫苗接种率提升过程中持续的错误信息和低信任度的问题。
设计与对象
在一项针对日本12至18岁未接种HPV疫苗的女儿的母亲的平行组随机临床试验中,比较了聊天机器人组和传单组在干预后立即和2周随访时的HPV疫苗接种知识的持续性。
核心方法
通过7个关于HPV及其疫苗接种的客观问题来测量HPV疫苗接种知识,主要结果在干预后立即和2周随访时进行评估。次要结果是在2周随访时的疫苗接种决定。
主要结果
聊天机器人组在干预后立即和2周随访时均显示出比传单组更高的HPV疫苗接种知识,但对疫苗接种决定没有显著影响。
解释与创新
本研究得出结论,AI驱动的HPV疫苗接种聊天机器人比政府传单更能有效提升HPV疫苗接种知识,其效果可持续至2周,但对疫苗接种决定没有显著影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对话式AI可能在提升HPV疫苗接种知识方面补充官方材料和医疗专业人员的建议。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括样本量较小和随访时间较短,这可能影响研究结果的普遍性。

AI Chatbot Use for Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Literacy in Japan

Authors: Kana Kobayashi, Zhengdong Wu, Hana Tomoi, Ken Masuda, Heidi J. Larson, Kathy Leung, Joseph T. Wu, Leesa Lin
Original abstract (English)
Importance Persistent misinformation and low confidence impede human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine uptake in Japan. Artificial intelligence (AI)–driven conversational agents may provide scalable vaccine education, but randomized evidence for HPV vaccine communication remains limited. Objective To determine whether an AI-driven HPV vaccine chatbot improves caregiver HPV vaccine literacy compared with a government leaflet. Design, Setting, and Participants Parallel-group randomized clinical trial (RCT) conducted through a national research panel of female caregivers in Japan with unvaccinated daughters aged 12 to 18 years from November 30 to December 27, 2024, with 2-week follow-up. Interventions Participants were randomized 1:1 to an AI-driven HPV vaccine chatbot or standard government HPV vaccine leaflet. Main outcomes and measures The primary outcome was an HPV vaccine literacy, evaluated immediately after intervention to estimate immediate intervention efficacy and evaluated at 2-week follow-up to estimate durability. HPV vaccine literacy was measured using 7 factual questions about HPV and HPV vaccination; scores are shown on a 0 to 7 scale, with higher scores indicating more correct responses.The secondary outcome was decision to vaccinate at 2-week follow-up. Results Among 848 randomized participants, 704 of 841 (83.7%) were included in the immediate modified intention-to-treat (mITT) analysis (353 chatbot, 351 leaflet) and 477 of 841 (56.7%) in the mITT analysis at 2 weeks (237 chatbot, 240 leaflet). A total of 447 of 703 participants (63.6%) were aged 40 to 49 years and had college or university education (472 of 703 participants [67.1%]). Immediately after the intervention, analysis of covariance-adjusted literacy was higher with the chatbot (adjusted mean difference, 0.30 points on the 0 to 7 scale; 95% CI, 0.18-0.43 points; P < .001). At the 2-week durability assessment, mean (SD) literacy increased by 0.40 (1.21) with the chatbot vs 0.28 (1.13) points with the leaflet; adjusted literacy again favored the chatbot (adjusted mean difference, 0.30 points; 95% CI, 0.13-0.47; P < .001). At 2 weeks, caregivers’ vaccination decisions did not differ between groups (102 of 253 participants [40.3%] vs 103 of 260 participants [39.6%]; adjusted odds ratio, 0.74; 95% CI, 0.41 to 1.33; P = .31). Conclusions and Relevance In this RCT of female caregivers in Japan, an AI-driven HPV vaccine chatbot produced higher adjusted HPV vaccine literacy than a government leaflet immediately after intervention and at 2-week follow-up, without a measurable short-term effect on vaccination decision. Conversational AI may complement official materials and health care professional recommendations. Trial Registration ClinicalTrials.gov Identifier: NCT06702423
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the issue of persistent misinformation and low confidence in HPV vaccine uptake in Japan by evaluating the efficacy of an AI-driven HPV vaccine chatbot compared to a government leaflet.
Design and population
A parallel-group randomized clinical trial was conducted among female caregivers in Japan with unvaccinated daughters aged 12 to 18 years, comparing the immediate and 2-week durability of HPV vaccine literacy between the chatbot and leaflet groups.
Core methods
HPV vaccine literacy was measured using 7 factual questions, and the primary outcome was assessed immediately after the intervention and at 2-week follow-up. The secondary outcome was the decision to vaccinate at 2 weeks.
Main results
The chatbot group showed higher HPV vaccine literacy immediately after the intervention and at 2-week follow-up compared to the leaflet group, with no measurable effect on vaccination decisions.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concluded that an AI-driven HPV vaccine chatbot improved HPV vaccine literacy more than a government leaflet, with effects lasting up to 2 weeks, but did not significantly impact vaccination decisions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that conversational AI may complement official materials and health care professional recommendations in enhancing HPV vaccine literacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the limited sample size and the short follow-up period, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

从HPV感染到宫颈癌的整个连续谱中阴道微生物群:一篇叙述性综述

The Vaginal Microbiome Across the Continuum from HPV Infection to Cervical Cancer: A Narrative Review
作者： Xiaodie Zhou, Jiaqi Hu, Ruoyu Zhou, Yi Lu, Xuan Du, Yipei Chen, Qingling Ren, Yue Han
摘要中文翻译持续的高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN)和侵袭性宫颈癌的核心前提。积累的转化证据证实,以保护性乳酸杆菌为主的阴道微生态平衡从根本上调控HR-HPV感染的启动、持续和自发清除,而以乳酸杆菌减少和厌氧病原体过度增殖为特征的微生物失调则会创造一个促炎、免疫抑制的黏膜微环境,加速病毒滞留和宫颈恶性转化。我们对2021年1月至2026年4月期间发表的主要研究进行了叙述性综述。通过PubMed、Web of Science、CNKI、万方数据和VIP数据库,使用与阴道微生物群、HR-HPV感染、CIN、宫颈癌、潜在机制、微生物生物标志物以及微生物群调节干预相关的关键词进行检索。优先选取了相关的同行评审流行病学、机制和临床研究,并参考了叙述性综述文章评估量表(SANRA)撰写本文。本综述系统阐述了在整个宫颈癌连续谱中阴道微生物群与HR-HPV之间的双向调控相互作用。微生物失调会破坏阴道酸性屏障,重塑局部细胞因子网络,损害抗病毒药物固有免疫,并调节HPV E6/E7致癌转录;反之,HR-HPV致癌蛋白则重塑微生物群落结构,维持一种失调的反馈循环。详细阐述了由失调微生物群引发的三种核心致癌级联反应:上皮屏障侵蚀、代谢重编程以及宿主和病毒基因组的表观遗传重排。梳理了与宫颈病变进展所有阶段相关的动态微生物特征,从短暂的HPV感染到高级别CIN和癌变。总结了针对微生物群的干预措施从实验室到临床的转化进展,包括单株/多株益生菌补充、工程化乳酸菌治疗疫苗、阴道微生物群移植以及能够重建阴道生态平衡的传统中医药疗法。展示了微生物多样性、菌株水平单核苷酸变异和分类学生物标志物在早期筛查、疗效预测和术后复发风险分层中的临床应用。以阴道微生物群为核心,这一全面框架阐明了HR-HPV沿整个路径至宫颈癌的进展内部机制,并提供了阻断这一级联反应的实用临床手段。当前的瓶颈包括益生菌标准不一致、临床前模型的局限性以及未解决的肠道-阴道微生物群相互作用,这些都需要在广泛临床转化之前进一步研究。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
持续的高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN)和侵袭性宫颈癌的核心前提。本综述探讨了在整个宫颈癌连续谱中阴道微生物群与HR-HPV之间的双向调控相互作用,重点分析了微生物失调、其对病毒感染的影响以及向宫颈癌发展的过程。
证据基础与方法
我们对2021年1月至2026年4月期间发表的主要研究进行了叙述性综述。通过PubMed、Web of Science、CNKI、万方数据和VIP数据库,使用与阴道微生物群、HR-HPV感染、CIN、宫颈癌、潜在机制、微生物生物标志物以及微生物群调节干预相关的关键词进行检索。优先选取了相关的同行评审流行病学、机制和临床研究,并参考了叙述性综述文章评估量表(SANRA)撰写本文。
共识与结论
微生物失调会破坏阴道酸性屏障,重塑局部细胞因子网络,损害抗病毒药物固有免疫,并调节HPV E6/E7致癌转录;反之,HR-HPV致癌蛋白则重塑微生物群落结构,维持一种失调的反馈循环。详细阐述了由失调微生物群引发的三种核心致癌级联反应:上皮屏障侵蚀、代谢重编程以及宿主和病毒基因组的表观遗传重排。梳理了与宫颈病变进展所有阶段相关的动态微生物特征,从短暂的HPV感染到高级别CIN和癌变。
争议与缺口
当前的瓶颈包括益生菌标准不一致、临床前模型的局限性以及未解决的肠道-阴道微生物群相互作用,这些都需要在广泛临床转化之前进一步研究。
科研与实践启示
总结了针对微生物群的干预措施从实验室到临床的转化进展,包括单株/多株益生菌补充、工程化乳酸菌治疗疫苗、阴道微生物群移植以及能够重建阴道生态平衡的传统中医药疗法。展示了微生物多样性、菌株水平单核苷酸变异和分类学生物标志物在早期筛查、疗效预测和术后复发风险分层中的临床应用。

The Vaginal Microbiome Across the Continuum from HPV Infection to Cervical Cancer: A Narrative Review

Authors: Xiaodie Zhou, Jiaqi Hu, Ruoyu Zhou, Yi Lu, Xuan Du, Yipei Chen, Qingling Ren, Yue Han
Original abstract (English)
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection serves as the core prerequisite for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) and invasive cervical cancer. Accumulated translational evidence confirms that vaginal microecological homeostasis dominated by protective Lactobacillus strains fundamentally governs the initiation, persistence, and spontaneous clearance of HR-HPV infection, while microbial dysbiosis featuring depleted lactobacilli and overgrown anaerobic pathogens creates a pro-inflammatory, immune-suppressive mucosal niche that accelerates viral retention and cervical malignant transformation. We conducted a narrative review of studies published primarily between January 2021 and April 2026. PubMed, Web of Science, CNKI, Wanfang Data, and VIP were searched using terms related to the vaginal microbiome, HR-HPV infection, CIN, cervical cancer, underlying mechanisms, microbial biomarkers, and microbiome-modulating interventions. Relevant peer-reviewed epidemiological, mechanistic, and clinical studies were prioritized, and the manuscript was prepared with reference to the Scale for the Assessment of Narrative Review Articles (SANRA). This review systematically delineates the bidirectional regulatory crosstalk between vaginal microbiota and HR-HPV across the cervical cancer continuum. Dysbiosis disrupts vaginal acidic barriers, reshapes local cytokine networks, impairs antiviral innate immunity, and modulates HPV E6/E7 oncogenic transcription; reciprocally, HR-HPV oncoproteins remodel microbial community structures to sustain a dysbiotic feedback loop. Three core carcinogenic cascades induced by dysbiotic flora are elaborated: epithelial barrier erosion, metabolic reprogramming, and epigenetic rewiring of host and viral genomes. Dynamic microbial characteristics corresponding to all stages of progressive cervical lesions, from transient HPV infection to high-grade CIN and carcinoma, are sorted out. The bench-to-bedside translational progress of microbiome-targeted interventions is summarized, including single/multi-strain probiotic supplementation, engineered lactic acid bacterial therapeutic vaccines, vaginal microbiota transplantation, and traditional Chinese medicine therapy that reconstructs vaginal eubiosis. The clinical application of microbial diversity, strain-level single-nucleotide variants, and taxonomic biomarkers for early screening, efficacy prediction, and postoperative recurrence risk stratification is illustrated. This comprehensive framework with the vaginal microbiome as the core clarifies the internal mechanism of HR-HPV progression along the entire pathway to cervical cancer and provides practical clinical means to block this cascade. Current bottlenecks, including inconsistent probiotic standards, preclinical model limitations, and unresolved gut–vagina microbiome interactions, require further investigation before widespread clinical translation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection serves as the core prerequisite for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) and invasive cervical cancer. This review explores the bidirectional regulatory crosstalk between vaginal microbiota and HR-HPV across the cervical cancer continuum, focusing on microbial dysbiosis, its impact on viral infection, and the progression to cervical cancer.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a narrative review of studies published primarily between January 2021 and April 2026. PubMed, Web of Science, CNKI, Wanfang Data, and VIP were searched using terms related to the vaginal microbiome, HR-HPV infection, CIN, cervical cancer, underlying mechanisms, microbial biomarkers, and microbiome-modulating interventions. Relevant peer-reviewed epidemiological, mechanistic, and clinical studies were prioritized, and the manuscript was prepared with reference to the Scale for the Assessment of Narrative Review Articles (SANRA).
Consensus and conclusions
Dysbiosis disrupts vaginal acidic barriers, reshapes local cytokine networks, impairs antiviral innate immunity, and modulates HPV E6/E7 oncogenic transcription; reciprocally, HR-HPV oncoproteins remodel microbial community structures to sustain a dysbiotic feedback loop. Three core carcinogenic cascades induced by dysbiotic flora are elaborated: epithelial barrier erosion, metabolic reprogramming, and epigenetic rewiring of host and viral genomes. Dynamic microbial characteristics corresponding to all stages of progressive cervical lesions, from transient HPV infection to high-grade CIN and carcinoma, are sorted out.
Controversies and gaps
Current bottlenecks, including inconsistent probiotic standards, preclinical model limitations, and unresolved gut–vagina microbiome interactions, require further investigation before widespread clinical translation.
Research and practice implications
The bench-to-bedside translational progress of microbiome-targeted interventions is summarized, including single/multi-strain probiotic supplementation, engineered lactic acid bacterial therapeutic vaccines, vaginal microbiota transplantation, and traditional Chinese medicine therapy that reconstructs vaginal eubiosis. The clinical application of microbial diversity, strain-level single-nucleotide variants, and taxonomic biomarkers for early screening, efficacy prediction, and postoperative recurrence risk stratification is illustrated.

当代全球人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染率及人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型分布情况:针对女性性工作者的更新系统综述和荟萃分析(2015–2025)

Contemporary global prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus among female sex workers, 2015–2025: an updated systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Mahdieh Abbasi, Javad Allahverdy, Mohammad Farahmand, Afsane Vaezi, Niloufar Rashidi, Bahar Mohamadi, Fariba Dorostkar
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 女性性工作者面临不成比例的HPV暴露风险,但HPV感染率和基因型分布的估计因地理区域、样本类型、诊断方法和基因型覆盖范围而异。本更新的系统综述和荟萃分析综合了2015年2月至2025年12月间发表的当代证据。方法 通过PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus和Web of Science进行系统检索,不限制语言。两名评审员独立筛选记录、提取数据,并使用JBI检查表对纳入研究进行批判性评价。为避免重复计数,每项研究人群保留一个独立的估计值。使用对数转换的随机效应广义线性混合模型综合总体和特定型别的HPV感染率估计值。分析了异质性、预测区间、亚组模式和敏感性分析。本综述未进行前瞻性注册。结果 从23个国家纳入了36个独立的研究/人群估计值,共包含12,193名女性性工作者,其中5,586名检测出HPV阳性。综合感染率为39.0%(95%置信区间,30.0%-48.9%),异质性非常高(I2=97.6%),预测区间较宽(5.0%-88.6%)。HPV感染率在世界银行地理区域、国家收入组别、诊断方法和样本类型之间存在显著差异。在至少有10项研究评估的基因型中,HPV-52的综合感染率最高,其次是HPV-53和HPV-16;HPV-58、HPV-35和HPV-66的综合感染率均为5.8%。敏感性分析未显著改变总体解释。结论 当前可用的证据表明,女性性工作者中HPV的总体负担相当大,尽管感染率在不同人群和环境中存在显著差异。广泛的基因型分布包括针对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的基因型和非人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的基因型,强调了针对该人群的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)筛查、系统发育分析以及整合的性传播疾病(STI)/艾滋病病毒(HIV)预防服务的重要性。

范围与问题
摘要 背景 女性性工作者面临不成比例的HPV暴露风险,但HPV感染率和基因型分布的估计因地理区域、样本类型、诊断方法和基因型覆盖范围而异。
证据基础与方法
本更新的系统综述和荟萃分析综合了2015年2月至2025年12月间发表的当代证据。
共识与结论
结果 从23个国家纳入了36个独立的研究/人群估计值,共包含12,193名女性性工作者,其中5,586名检测出HPV阳性。
争议与缺口
分析了异质性、预测区间、亚组模式和敏感性分析。
科研与实践启示
广泛的基因型分布包括针对人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的基因型和非人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的基因型,强调了针对该人群的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)筛查、系统发育分析以及整合的性传播疾病(STI)/艾滋病病毒(HIV)预防服务的重要性。

Contemporary global prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus among female sex workers, 2015–2025: an updated systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Mahdieh Abbasi, Javad Allahverdy, Mohammad Farahmand, Afsane Vaezi, Niloufar Rashidi, Bahar Mohamadi, Fariba Dorostkar
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Female sex workers experience disproportionate exposure to human papillomavirus (HPV), but estimates of HPV prevalence and genotype distribution vary according to geography, specimen type, diagnostic method, and genotype coverage. This updated systematic review and meta-analysis synthesized contemporary evidence published between February 2015 and December 2025. Methods PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, and Web of Science were systematically searched without language restrictions. Two reviewers independently screened records, extracted data, and critically appraised included studies using the JBI checklist for prevalence studies. One independent estimate was retained per study population for the primary analysis to avoid double-counting. Overall and type-specific HPV prevalence estimates were synthesized using random-effects generalized linear mixed models with logit transformation. Heterogeneity, prediction intervals, subgroup patterns, and sensitivity analyses were examined. The review was not prospectively registered. Results Thirty-six independent study/population estimates from 23 countries included 12,193 female sex workers, of whom 5,586 were HPV positive. The pooled HPV prevalence was 39.0% (95% confidence interval, 30.0%-48.9%), with very high heterogeneity (I2=97.6%) and a wide prediction interval (5.0%-88.6%). Prevalence varied across World Bank geographic regions, country income groups, diagnostic methods, and specimen types. Among genotypes assessed in at least 10 studies, HPV-52 had the highest pooled prevalence, followed by HPV-53 and HPV-16; HPV-58, HPV-35, and HPV-66 each had a pooled prevalence of 5.8%. Sensitivity analyses did not materially change the overall interpretation. Conclusions The available contemporary evidence indicates a substantial overall burden of HPV among female sex workers, although prevalence varies markedly across populations and settings. The broad genotype distribution includes both vaccine-targeted and non-vaccine types and underscores the relevance of vaccination, cervical cancer screening, and integrated STI/HIV prevention services for this population.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

人乳头瘤病毒相关口咽部鳞状细胞癌与持续性口腔高危型HPV检测:关于纵向评估、诊断评估及监测的叙述性综述

HPV-Associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma and Persistent Oral High-Risk HPV Detection: A Narrative Review of Longitudinal Assessment, Diagnostic Evaluation, and Surveillance
作者： Aornrutai Promsong, Wipawee Nittayananta
摘要中文翻译目前,尚无经过验证的基于人群的筛查策略利用持续性口腔高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)检测来识别具有临床显著风险的人乳头瘤病毒相关口咽部鳞状细胞癌(OPSCC)的个体。本叙述性综述评估了三种临床情境下的采样策略、病毒学检测方法、液体活检生物标志物、辅助生物标志物及整合方法:纵向口腔HPV评估;疑似或新诊断OPSCC中的诊断评估和HPV归因;以及在已确诊的人乳头瘤病毒相关OPSCC中治疗监测和术后监测。从2020年1月至2026年6月在PubMed中发表的文献被按临床情境、样本来源、生物靶标、研究人群和预期用途进行综合分析。在连续的口腔样本中重复检测到相同的高危型HPV基因型可作为持续性口腔HPV检测的实用指标,但不能证明持续感染、病毒癌基因持续转录、恶性转化或未来OPSCC风险。口腔HPV DNA、口腔E6/E7 mRNA、肿瘤组织HPV检测和血浆循环肿瘤HPV DNA代表不同的生物和临床信号。血浆循环肿瘤HPV DNA在已确诊的人乳头瘤病毒相关OPSCC中治疗监测和术后监测方面具有最成熟的证据,但不适用于人群筛查。临床转化需要标准化的纵向协议、在预期用途人群中进行验证、可操作的管理路径以及对拟议风险分层模型的增量预测价值。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
目前,尚无经过验证的基于人群的筛查策略利用持续性口腔高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)检测来识别具有临床显著风险的人乳头瘤病毒相关口咽部鳞状细胞癌(OPSCC)的个体。
证据基础与方法
从2020年1月至2026年6月在PubMed中发表的文献被按临床情境、样本来源、生物靶标、研究人群和预期用途进行综合分析。
共识与结论
在连续的口腔样本中重复检测到相同的高危型HPV基因型可作为持续性口腔HPV检测的实用指标,但不能证明持续感染、病毒癌基因持续转录、恶性转化或未来OPSCC风险。
争议与缺口
口腔HPV DNA、口腔E6/E7 mRNA、肿瘤组织HPV检测和血浆循环肿瘤HPV DNA代表不同的生物和临床信号。
科研与实践启示
临床转化需要标准化的纵向协议、在预期用途人群中进行验证、可操作的管理路径以及对拟议风险分层模型的增量预测价值。

HPV-Associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma and Persistent Oral High-Risk HPV Detection: A Narrative Review of Longitudinal Assessment, Diagnostic Evaluation, and Surveillance

Authors: Aornrutai Promsong, Wipawee Nittayananta
Original abstract (English)
Currently, no validated population-based screening strategy uses persistent oral high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) detection to identify individuals at clinically meaningful risk of HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC). This narrative review evaluates sampling strategies, virologic assays, liquid-biopsy biomarkers, adjunct biomarkers, and integrative approaches across three clinical contexts: longitudinal oral HPV assessment; diagnostic evaluation and HPV attribution in suspected or newly diagnosed OPSCC; and treatment monitoring and post-treatment surveillance in established HPV-associated OPSCC. The literature published from January 2020 through June 2026 and identified in PubMed was synthesized by clinical context, specimen source, biological target, study population, and intended use. Repeated detection of the same high-risk HPV genotype in serial oral specimens provides a pragmatic measure of persistent oral HPV detection but does not establish uninterrupted infection, sustained viral oncogene transcription, malignant transformation, or future OPSCC risk. Oral HPV DNA, oral E6/E7 mRNA, tumor-tissue HPV testing, and plasma circulating tumor HPV DNA represent distinct biological and clinical signals. Plasma circulating tumor HPV DNA has the most mature evidence for treatment monitoring and post-treatment surveillance in established HPV-associated OPSCC, not population screening. Clinical translation requires standardized longitudinal protocols, validation in intended-use populations, actionable management pathways, and incremental predictive value for proposed risk-stratification models.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Currently, no validated population-based screening strategy uses persistent oral high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) detection to identify individuals at clinically meaningful risk of HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC).
Evidence base and method
The literature published from January 2020 through June 2026 and identified in PubMed was synthesized by clinical context, specimen source, biological target, study population, and intended use.
Consensus and conclusions
Repeated detection of the same high-risk HPV genotype in serial oral specimens provides a pragmatic measure of persistent oral HPV detection but does not establish uninterrupted infection, sustained viral oncogene transcription, malignant transformation, or future OPSCC risk.
Controversies and gaps
Oral HPV DNA, oral E6/E7 mRNA, tumor-tissue HPV testing, and plasma circulating tumor HPV DNA represent distinct biological and clinical signals.
Research and practice implications
Clinical translation requires standardized longitudinal protocols, validation in intended-use populations, actionable management pathways, and incremental predictive value for proposed risk-stratification models.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

超越宫颈阴道局限:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染与口腔、肛门和阴茎部位黏膜微生物群之间的潜在双向相互作用——简要综述

Beyond cervicovaginal limitations: potential bidirectional interactions between HPV infection and mucosal microbiota from oral, anorectal and penile sites — a mini-review
作者： Wei Liu, Qiuyang Yu, Xue Xiao, Xiao Xue
摘要中文翻译目前大多数关于HPV-微生物群相互作用的研究局限于宫颈阴道部位,而口腔、肛门和阴茎部位黏膜微生物群的相互作用仍缺乏充分总结。本简要综述通过叙述性综合,总结了现有关于HPV感染与口腔、肛门和阴茎部位特定黏膜微生物群之间潜在双向相互作用的测序和流行病学证据。口腔HPV感染会以性别、种族和人群依赖的方式改变微生物组成;牙周失调、高危型HPV和白色念珠菌共同形成一个三元致癌轴,可能与口腔鳞状细胞癌相关,且牙周炎症独立增加了口腔HPV的易感性。在男同性恋者中,与肛门HPV相关的病变中积累了如脆弱拟杆菌和斯奈特氏菌等促炎厌氧菌,同时并发HIV或性传播细菌感染会加剧黏膜失调和病毒持续感染。在阴茎皮肤上,棒状杆菌与较低的高危型HPV风险相关,而以厌氧菌为主的社区状态类型则与较高的高危型HPV风险相关,HIV引起的基线失调可能掩盖了与HPV特异性相关的阴茎微生物特征。总体而言,特定部位的黏膜失调可能代表与持续HPV感染及随后恶性转化相关的重要共因子。本综述弥补了长期存在的性别相关和解剖学研究偏见,阐明了多部位HPV-病毒-微生物机制,并为开发基于微生物群的生物标志物和微生态干预措施以预防非宫颈HPV相关恶性肿瘤提供了理论支持。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述探讨了人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染与口腔、肛门和阴茎部位黏膜微生物群之间的双向相互作用,重点分析这些相互作用如何影响HPV的持续感染和恶性转化。核心问题是:微生物群的变化是否在宫颈阴道以外的部位也与HPV相关疾病的发生有关?
证据基础与方法
本综述是关于HPV与黏膜微生物群相互作用的测序和流行病学证据的叙述性综合。它未报告特定数据库、搜索日期或元分析方法,而是总结了多个来源关于该主题的研究发现。
共识与结论
有共识认为,口腔HPV感染会以性别、种族和人群依赖的方式改变微生物组成。牙周失调、高危型HPV和白色念珠菌共同形成的三元致癌轴可能与口腔鳞状细胞癌相关。此外,牙周炎症独立增加了口腔HPV的易感性。在男同性恋者中,与肛门HPV相关的病变与促炎厌氧菌如脆弱拟杆菌和斯奈特氏菌有关,而HIV或细菌共感染会加剧黏膜失调。在阴茎皮肤上,以厌氧菌为主的社区状态类型与较高的高危型HPV风险相关,而HIV引起的失调可能掩盖了与HPV特异性相关的微生物特征。
争议与缺口
由于HIV引起的失调可能掩盖了阴茎部位HPV感染特有的微生物特征,目前的证据在充分描述这些部位HPV感染的微生物特征方面存在局限。此外,关于非宫颈部位HPV相关恶性肿瘤中微生物群作用的全面数据仍缺乏,特别是在多样化人群中。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应着重于阐明阴茎部位HPV感染特有的微生物特征,并考虑HIV引起的失调影响。此外,还需要更多关于非宫颈部位HPV相关恶性肿瘤中微生物群作用的研究,特别是针对多样化人群。这些发现可能支持开发基于微生物群的生物标志物和干预措施,以预防非宫颈HPV相关癌症。

Beyond cervicovaginal limitations: potential bidirectional interactions between HPV infection and mucosal microbiota from oral, anorectal and penile sites — a mini-review

Authors: Wei Liu, Qiuyang Yu, Xue Xiao, Xiao Xue
Original abstract (English)
Most existing studies focusing on human papillomavirus (HPV)-microbiota interactions are confined to the cervicovaginal tract, while mucosal microecological crosstalk at oral, anorectal and penile sites remains insufficiently summarized. This mini-review provides a narrative synthesis of current sequencing and epidemiological evidence regarding potential bidirectional interactions between HPV infection and site-specific mucosal microbiota across extra-cervicovaginal niches in both males and females. Oral HPV reshapes microbial composition in a sex-, race- and population-dependent manner; periodontal dysbiosis, high-risk HPV and Candida albicans jointly form a tripartite oncogenic axis that may contribute to oral squamous cell carcinoma, and periodontal inflammation independently elevates oral HPV susceptibility. Among men who have sex with men, pro-inflammatory anaerobes such as Bacteroides fragilis and Sneathia accumulate in anorectal HPV-related lesions, and concurrent HIV or sexually transmitted bacterial co-infections worsen mucosal dysbiosis and viral persistence. On penile skin, Corynebacterium is associated with a lower prevalence of HPV, whereas anaerobe-dominant community state types increase high-risk HPV risk, with HIV-induced baseline dysbiosis masking HPV-specific penile microbial signatures. Collectively, tissue-specific mucosal dysbiosis may represent an important co-factor linked to persistent HPV infection and subsequent malignant transformation. This review offsets long-standing sex-related and anatomical research bias, clarifies multi-site host-virus-microbe mechanisms, and provides theoretical support for developing microbiota-based biomarkers and microecological interventions to prevent non-cervical HPV-associated malignancies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review explores the bidirectional interactions between human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and mucosal microbiota at oral, anorectal, and penile sites, with a focus on how these interactions may influence HPV persistence and malignant transformation. The central question is whether microbiota alterations contribute to HPV-related diseases beyond the cervicovaginal tract.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of sequencing and epidemiological evidence from studies on HPV and mucosal microbiota interactions. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods, but it summarizes findings from multiple sources on the topic.
Consensus and conclusions
There is consensus that oral HPV infection alters microbial composition in a sex-, race-, and population-dependent manner. A tripartite oncogenic axis involving periodontal dysbiosis, high-risk HPV, and Candida albicans is implicated in oral squamous cell carcinoma. Additionally, periodontal inflammation independently increases susceptibility to oral HPV. In men who have sex with men, anorectal HPV-related lesions are associated with pro-inflammatory anaerobes like Bacteroides fragilis and Sneathia, and HIV or bacterial co-infections exacerbate mucosal dysbiosis. On penile skin, anaerobe-dominant community state types are linked to increased high-risk HPV risk, while HIV-induced dysbiosis may obscure HPV-specific microbial signatures.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited in its ability to fully characterize the microbiota signatures specific to HPV infection at penile sites due to the masking effect of HIV-induced dysbiosis. There is also a lack of comprehensive data on the role of microbiota in HPV-associated malignancies at non-cervicovaginal sites, particularly in diverse populations.
Research and practice implications
Future research should focus on elucidating the microbiota signatures specific to HPV infection in penile sites, accounting for HIV-induced dysbiosis. Additionally, there is a need for more studies on the role of microbiota in HPV-associated malignancies at non-cervicovaginal sites, with an emphasis on diverse populations. These findings could support the development of microbiota-based biomarkers and interventions for preventing non-cervical HPV-related cancers.

Lived experiences, reproductive concerns, and unmet supportive care needs among reproductive-aged women with persistent HPV infection in China: a qualitative study

Lived experiences, reproductive concerns, and unmet supportive care needs among reproductive-aged women with persistent HPV infection in China: a qualitative study

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

PDS Biotech Announces Publication of VERSATILE-002 Phase 2 Clinical Trial Results of PDS0101 with pembrolizumab in HPV16-Positive Recurrent and/or Metastatic (R/M) Head and Neck Cancer in JAMA Oncology - manilatimes.net

PDS Biotech Announces Publication of VERSATILE-002 Phase 2 Clinical Trial Results of PDS0101 with pembrolizumab in HPV16-Positive Recurrent and/or Metastatic (R/M) Head and Neck Cancer in JAMA Oncology - manilatimes.net
新闻简报PDS Biotech announced the publication of Phase 2 trial results for PDS0101, an HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, in combination with pembrolizumab for treating HPV16-positive recurrent and/or metastatic head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). The trial, conducted at 26 oncology centers across the US, UK, and Ireland, involved 88 patients, with 75 in the modified intention-to-treat (mITT) population. The median overall survival (mOS) for ICI-naïve participants was 39.3 months, surpassing the 17.9 months reported for standard of care pembrolizumab. The objective response rate (ORR) was 36%, with 5/53 complete responders by investigator review and 6/53 by central review. The treatment was well-tolerated, with 13.8% Grade ≥3 treatment-related adverse events (TRAEs) and one Grade 4 event observed one year post-therapy. The study was non-randomized and led by Dr. Jared Weiss, MD, at the Lineberger Comprehensive Cancer Center. The results are considered preliminary, and no head-to-head trials have been conducted. The Company continues to focus on PDS0301 for metastatic colorectal cancer while exploring partnerships for PDS0101.
查看新闻五要素
时间
The Phase 2 trial results were published on September 17, 2026, with the news announcement made on September 22, 2026. The trial was conducted over a period that included one year of follow-up for adverse events.
地点与对象
该试验在美国、英国和爱尔兰的26家肿瘤中心进行,纳入了88名经组织学确诊的R/M HPV16阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)患者,其中75名属于修改后的意向治疗(mITT)人群,53名未曾接受免疫检查点抑制剂(ICI)治疗。
事件
PDS Biotech announced the publication of Phase 2 clinical trial results for PDS0101, an HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, in combination with pembrolizumab for treating HPV16-positive recurrent and/or metastatic head and neck cancer. The trial was non-randomized and led by Dr.
规模、影响与风险
该试验报告了未曾接受免疫检查点抑制剂(ICI)治疗的患者的中位总生存期(mOS)为39.3个月,显著高于标准治疗帕博利珠单抗的17.9个月。对于CPS ≥ 1的患者,客观缓解率(ORR)为36%,其中根据研究者评估有5/53例完全缓解,根据中央评估有6/53例完全缓解。治疗耐受性良好,治疗后一年观察到13.8%的≥3级治疗相关不良事件(TRAEs),并记录了一例4级事件。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究发表于《JAMA肿瘤学》这本同行评审期刊,其结果被认为是初步的。公司继续优先推进PDS0301在转移性结直肠癌中的应用,同时探索与PDS0101的合作机会。尚未进行头对头试验,研究结果基于非随机数据。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

PDS Biotech Announces Publication of VERSATILE-002 Phase 2 Clinical Trial Results of PDS0101 with pembrolizumab in HPV16-Positive Recurrent and/or Metastatic (R/M) Head and Neck Cancer in JAMA Oncology - manilatimes.net

News Brief
PDS Biotech announced the publication of Phase 2 trial results for PDS0101, an HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, in combination with pembrolizumab for treating HPV16-positive recurrent and/or metastatic head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). The trial, conducted at 26 oncology centers across the US, UK, and Ireland, involved 88 patients, with 75 in the modified intention-to-treat (mITT) population. The median overall survival (mOS) for ICI-naïve participants was 39.3 months, surpassing the 17.9 months reported for standard of care pembrolizumab. The objective response rate (ORR) was 36%, with 5/53 complete responders by investigator review and 6/53 by central review. The treatment was well-tolerated, with 13.8% Grade ≥3 treatment-related adverse events (TRAEs) and one Grade 4 event observed one year post-therapy. The study was non-randomized and led by Dr. Jared Weiss, MD, at the Lineberger Comprehensive Cancer Center. The results are considered preliminary, and no head-to-head trials have been conducted. The Company continues to focus on PDS0301 for metastatic colorectal cancer while exploring partnerships for PDS0101.
Original source text (English)
39.3 months median overall survival in first-line (1L) R/M immune checkpoint inhibitor naïve patients Company continues to prioritize PDS0301 in metastatic colorectal cancer (mCRC) while pursuing partnership strategy for PDS0101 PRINCETON, N.J., Sept. 22, 2026 (GLOBE NEWSWIRE) -- PDS Biotechnology Corporation (Nasdaq: PDSB) ("PDS Biotech” or the "Company”), a late-stage immunotherapy company focused on transforming how the immune system targets and kills cancers, today announced the publication of results from a Phase 2 trial evaluating PDS0101, its HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, in combination with pembrolizumab in the September 17, 2026, issue of JAMA Oncology , a peer reviewed journal of the American Medical Association . The data were published in an article titled " VERSATILE-002 - A Phase 2 Study of PDS0101 HPV16-targeted T Cell Immunotherapy and Pembrolizumab Combination in HPV16-Positive Recurrent/Metastatic Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Nonrandomized Clinical Trial . ” Key findings Get the latest news delivered to your inbox Sign up for The Manila Times newsletters By signing up with an email address, I acknowledge that I have read and agree to the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy . 62.3% of the ICI-naïve participants in the mITT cohort (n = 53) had low CPS of 1-19, and 37.7% had high CPS ≥ 20. In the overall CPS ≥ 1 population, the mOS was 39.3 months (95% CI, 23.9 to NE); The best published result with standard of care pembrolizumab or pembrolizumab + chemotherapy is 17.9 months 1. Objective response rate (ORR) for CPS ≥ 1 was 36% of which 5/53 were complete responders by investigator review and 34% of which 6/53 were complete responders by central review. The published ORR for the overall population with CPS ≥ 1 when treated with standard of care pembrolizumab is 19%2. The treatment was well-tolerated with 13.8% Grade ≥3 treatment-related adverse events (TRAEs), 1 Grade 4 event one year after PDS0101 therapy, and no Grade 5 TRAEs. The published safety profile for the overall population with CPS ≥ 1 when treated with standard of care pembrolizumab reports 17% Grade ≥ 3 TRAEs2. VERSATILE-002 was an open-label, non-randomized phase 2 trial performed at twenty-six oncology centers across the US, UK and Ireland. Eighty-eight patients with histologically confirmed R/M HPV16-positive diseases were enrolled in the study; 87 received treatment and comprised the safety population; 75 were in the modified Intention-to-Treat (mITT) population used for efficacy analyses: 53 ICI-naïve with PD-L1 CPS ≥1, 22 ICI-resistant (Mean age = 64.5 years, 94.7% male, 92.0% White). The trial was led by Dr. Jared Weiss, MD, of the Lineberger Comprehensive Cancer Center, University of North Carolina, Chapel Hill, NC, and was performed to determine the efficacy and safety of PDS0101, in combination with pembrolizumab in participants with HPV16-positive recurrent or metastatic head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (R/M HNSCC), with separate evaluation of immune checkpoint inhibitor (ICI)-naïve and -resistant cohorts. "We’re very pleased with the publication of the Phase 2 clinical results of the VERSATILE-002 clinical trial in a leading peer reviewed oncology journal,” said Dr. Kirk Shepard, M.D., Chief Medical Officer of PDS Biotech. "These results support the durable clinical effect of PDS0101, consistent with promising survival outcomes reported in two other recently published studies, the IMMUNOCERV study, and the NCI-led study in HPV16-positive recurrent and/or metastatic cancers. We believe PDS0101 has the potential to bring new hope to the rapidly growing population of HPV16-positive head and neck cancer patients.” The Company recently announced the initial closing of a PIPE financing of up to $22.3 million led by Nant Capital, alongside an agreement granting NantWorks, LLC, an affiliate of Nant Capital, a one-year exclusive right to negotiate an exclusive license to PDS0101. *No head-to-head trials have been performed. 1 Licitra L et al, J Clin Oncol; 2026 Apr 20;44(12):1098-1107.doi: 10.1200/JCO-25-00570. Epub 2026 Mar 12 2 Harrington, KJ et al. J Clin Oncol. 2022;41:790-802. https://doi.org/10.1200/JCO.21.02508 . About PDS Biotechnology PDS Biotechnology is a clinical-stage biotechnology company focused on developing targeted immunotherapies for cancer. PDS Biotechnology owns the Versamune® cancer vaccine platform, including PDS0101, which has been evaluated in HPV16-positive cancers. The Company intends to pursue strategic partnerships or other externally funded opportunities for the Phase 3 development of PDS0101. The Company’s lead development program, PDS0301 (also referred to as PDS01ADC & NHS-IL12), is an investigational tumor-targeted interleukin-12 (IL-12) immunocytokine designed to deliver IL-12 preferentially to the tumor microenvironment, with the goal of enhancing anti-tumor immune activity while limiting systemic exposure. PDS0301 has been clinically evaluated across multiple solid tumors, including metastatic colorectal cancer and prostate cancer. The Company is focused on advancing PDS0301 in indications where its tumor-targeted mechanism may help address and overcome mechanisms of resistance to immunotherapy. View five news elements
Time
The Phase 2 trial results were published on September 17, 2026, with the news announcement made on September 22, 2026. The trial was conducted over a period that included one year of follow-up for adverse events.
Location and population
The trial was conducted at twenty-six oncology centers across the US, UK, and Ireland. It involved 88 patients with histologically confirmed R/M HPV16-positive head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC), with 75 in the modified Intention-to-Treat (mITT) population and 53 ICI-naïve participants.
Event
PDS Biotech announced the publication of Phase 2 clinical trial results for PDS0101, an HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, in combination with pembrolizumab for treating HPV16-positive recurrent and/or metastatic head and neck cancer. The trial was non-randomized and led by Dr.
Scale, impact and risk
The trial reported a median overall survival (mOS) of 39.3 months for ICI-naïve participants, which is significantly higher than the 17.9 months reported for standard of care pembrolizumab. The objective response rate (ORR) was 36% for CPS ≥ 1, with 5/53 complete responders by investigator review and 6/53 by central review. The treatment was well-tolerated, with 13.8% Grade ≥3 treatment-related adverse events (TRAEs) and one Grade 4 event observed one year after therapy.
Response, status and uncertainty
The trial was published in JAMA Oncology, a peer-reviewed journal, and the results are considered preliminary. The Company continues to prioritize PDS0301 in metastatic colorectal cancer while pursuing partnerships for PDS0101. No head-to-head trials have been performed, and the findings are based on non-randomized data.
